## R2 — §4 + §13 — the end-to-end lifecycle (Describe → … → Run forever) and what the output IS

**Assessor's region summary:** R2 (§4 the end-to-end lifecycle, §13 what the output IS), measured against the live backend at 127.0.0.1:8031 by executing a full Genesis journey, a direct /establish, a /vsb/spawn, the review-gate decision flow, and the repo/website/webapp/mobile/zip/file generators on three entities I created, then reading the generated repositories on disk and the React components that render them.  The honest headline: §4's gated lifecycle is real where Genesis drives it. A pending Mode-3 review gate refused six separate movers with HTTP 409 and a named reason, refused the establish-time ship of itself, and released all four generators on approval. The repository is a genuine git repo with real commits, a working zip download, a traversal-safe file endpoint, and a three-page static site, a client-side web app and an installable PWA that are real source. Disclosure on the floor-served path is the best I have seen in this system: the Genesis page puts an amber "the deterministic native floor served 15 of these calls — not generative model reasoning" card ABOVE the results, every stage verification returns `verified: null` with a basis saying the proxies cannot fail, the ranking volunteers "All 3 candidates returned IDENTICAL text, so no comparison was possible", the shipped Business Plan says "content pending the owned model", and so does the generated public website. "No model ran" is the environment; the system says so honestly and that is tier 3, not a defect.  The gap is a vocabulary that five readers did not follow. W496/W573 replaced literal `status: "operational"` and `stage: "commercialise"` with derived values, and the derivations are good — but the empty-body case was missed, so an entity with three blank §4 sections ships a README asserting "every §4 section on this entity is composed"; the spawn stream's final event still says "VSB Operational" over a record it saved as "body pending"; the Organism page's green "1 operational" chip and its 8% readiness figure still count the two abolished words and can never count the new one; and the spawn stream still says "Chief twin" three lines under a comment disclaiming one. Separately, ship_vsb_repo writes the repo manifest before the three surface generators run and never rewrites it, so every establish-shipped entity's manifest reports 13 of 29 files, calls all three §13 surfaces "(scaffold)" minutes after generating them, sets stale=false, and attaches a count_basis swearing it counted every file on disk — and that manifest is exactly what the Cockpit's repo browser shows the founder.  On the W434 question: zero of the ten §4 stages contain the user's own problem SOLVED. Seven of seven stage bodies are the engine's scaffold over bigrams extracted from the request; the only two steps that read the problem rather than counting its words are the §11 keyword screen and the keyword-driven derived-stage selector. The whole "Concept → Commercialisation cascade" completed in 0.78 seconds. And there is not ONE lifecycle: /vsb and /vsb-spawn route to a second establishment page that runs none of §4 and configures no gates, while /api/v1/genesis/establish is a third entrance that accepts an entity with no bodies at all.  THE CAP IS TEN AND THIS IS NOT THE SIZE OF THE GAP. Items I verified or part-verified and could not fit: the board pack and marketplace listing paths; the VSB economy waterfall and virtual-WST books; §4.9's own list of eight output kinds against the two buttons (Report, Presentation) the Genesis page actually offers; §4.1's multimodal claim against an attach control whose `accept` list admits text, JSON and PDF but no image; the floor emitting a heading truncated mid-word ("## Problem Understandi") and an empty "## Key Steps" into delivered text; the Business Plan printing the founder's problem statement verbatim under "## Vision"; and a malformed VSB record on disk (vsb-0a6ca2ab.json, vsb_id and name both null) that no store validator rejected. Read ten as the ten most consequential, not as all there was.

### R2.0 · The shipped repo manifest reports 13 of 29 files and calls the generated Website, Web app and Phone app "(scaffold)" — and swears it counted every file on disk — **PARTIAL** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-a42ca383af/repo returns `file_count: 13`, `total_bytes: 10901`, a 13-entry `tree`, `integrated_surfaces: {website: "web/index.html (scaffold)", webapp: "webapp/ (scaffold)", mobile: "mobile/ (scaffold)"}`, `ship_status.stale: false` — and a `count_basis` that states in words "every file on disk under the repository root except .git and this manifest". 29 files are on disk. The sixteen missing ones are the founder's entire §13 output: web/about.html, web/solution.html, web/styles.css, web/site.json, webapp/index.html, webapp/app.js, webapp/data.json, mobile/index.html, mobile/sw.js, mobile/icon.svg and the rest. This is not an omission — `count_basis` asserts the count is complete and `integrated_surfaces` asserts three directories are scaffolds that were generated five seconds later, so a reader is told something untrue rather than merely left uninformed. Not tier 2 because VSBCockpit.tsx:238 feeds exactly this `tree` into the repo browser a founder clicks, so the untrue list is the surface itself, not a hidden field.
- **claim:** §13 — the canonical output is a Repository of the IDBO Entity shipped as a coherent, version-controlled whole that integrates a Website, a Web app and a Phone (mobile) app.
- **observed:** ship_vsb_repo (agentic_core/api/vsb.py:368) runs its generators in the order repo → website → webapp → mobile → board_pack. generate_vsb_repo writes manifest.json FIRST, while `_generated = {website: (root/'web'/'site.json').exists(), ...}` is still all False and only 13 files exist. The three surface generators then write 16 more files and their own manifests; nothing ever re-writes the repo manifest. So every entity shipped at establish time carries a permanently 16-file-short manifest that labels all three §13 surfaces as scaffolds, with stale=false. Re-POSTing /repo by hand afterwards fixes it (vsb-9313233561 then reported file_count 27 and "web/ (generated — index · about · solution)"), which is how I isolated the ordering.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/establish -> vsb-a42ca383af, initial_ship {shipped:true, coherent_whole:true, surfaces:[board_pack,mobile,repo,webapp,website], commit 9f69db933014}. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-a42ca383af/repo -> file_count 13, total_bytes 10901, integrated_surfaces all "(scaffold)", ship_status {stale:false, shipped_at 2026-10-05T14:47:28Z}, manifest generated_at 14:47:23Z. `find C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_repos/vsb-a42ca383af -type f -not -path ./.git/* -not -name manifest.json | wc -l` -> 29. `git log --oneline` in that root -> 4 commits (9f69db9 mobile, f281f28 webapp, 6d86547 website, 341aeb5 repo). Reader: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/VSBCockpit.tsx:238-239 and :1195 (`repoTree.map`).
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the opposite. ship_status.stale is false and count_basis claims a complete disk count.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). CONFIRMED, tier 1 held, and the finding UNDERSTATES its own scope in two ways I corrected. (a) The assessor's mechanism is wrong: agentic_core/api/vsb.py:428-432 does count from disk (`for _p in sorted(root.rglob('*'))`), not from `written` — the defect is purely the ship ORDERING at vsb.py:2406-2407 (repo generator first, then website/webapp/mobile write 16 more files and nothing re-writes the repo manifest). (b) Far more consequential: the assessor said re-POSTing /repo afterwards fixes it and implied this is an establish-time-only artefact. It is not. I ran POST /{id}/repo/ship — §13's own canonical 'one deliberate act' route — on a cleanly gated entity and the manifest came back 13 files again, because the ship re-runs the repo generator first every single time. So EVERY path the system offers produces the short manifest; only a hand-crafted bare POST /repo after a ship repairs it. Tier 1 rather than 2: `count_basis` asserts in words 'every file on disk under the repository root except .git and this manifest', `integrated_surfaces` asserts three directories are scaffolds when generated apps are in them, `x-repo-missing: none` asserts nothing is absent, and the Cockpit's own button reads 'Download all 13 file(s) as a zip' on a 29-file repository — four affirmative statements, each untrue, none an omission. Tier 1 rather than 3 because the manifest tree is also the download ALLOW-LIST (vsb.py:_repo_resolved_path), so the founder's website and apps are not merely unlisted, they are unreachable by file and absent from the zip — the surface both misstates and withholds. This is the most consequential finding in R2.
- **refuter's evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/establish (no bodies) -> vsb-cdc8805381, initial_ship {shipped:true, coherent_whole:true, surfaces:[board_pack,mobile,repo,webapp,website], commit e434616ebc48}. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-cdc8805381/repo -> file_count 13, total_bytes 10181, a 13-path tree, integrated_surfaces {website:'web/index.html (scaffold)', webapp:'webapp/ (scaffold)', mobile:'mobile/ (scaffold)'}, ship_status {stale:false}. Walked C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_repos/vsb-cdc8805381 excluding .git and manifest.json -> 29 files, 72703 bytes (web/about.html, web/solution.html, web/site.json, web/styles.css, webapp/index.html, webapp/app.js, webapp/app.json, webapp/data.json, webapp/styles.css, mobile/index.html, mobile/app.js, mobile/app.json, mobile/data.json, mobile/styles.css, mobile/sw.js, mobile/icon.svg all absent from the manifest). GET /repo/file?path=web/about.html -> 404 "'web/about.html' is not a file this repo declares ... 13 file(s) are declared." GET /repo/zip -> 200, content-length 7361, x-repo-files 13, x-repo-declared 13, x-repo-missing: none. SECOND, HARDER REPRODUCTION on the explicit canonical act: vsb-5f26b5c0c4, gate approved, POST /repo/ship -> 200 {shipped:true, coherent_whole:true, surfaces_shipped: all five} and GET /repo STILL reports file_count 13, integrated_surfaces all '(scaffold)', zip content-length 8089 / x-repo-files 13. Renderer: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/VSBCockpit.tsx:1163 button text 'Download all {repoTree.length} file(s) as a zip' and :1195 repoTree.map.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** In ship_vsb_repo (agentic_core/api/vsb.py:2402), move "repo" to the END of the generator tuple (or re-run generate_vsb_repo after the loop), so the manifest the Cockpit reads is written against the finished disk and `_generated` is true when integrated_surfaces is computed.

### R2.1 · An entity established with no concept, design or commercialisation ships a README saying every §4 section is composed — **STUB** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The shipped README.md and the VSB Cockpit both print: "**Stage:** commercialisation" and "**Stage basis:** the furthest §4 section composed with no gap before it: commercialisation (every §4 section on this entity is composed)". In the same repository, IDENTITY.md shows `"concept": "", "design": "", "commercialisation": ""` and BUSINESS_PLAN.md has three empty headings. Zero §4 sections are composed; the sentence says all of them are. That is an affirmative false statement about the entity's own state, not a blank field, so it is not the "omission is not an assertion" case; and it is not tier 2 because nothing anywhere qualifies it — the two honest files in the same repo (OPERATIONS.md, EVIDENCE.md) say "Not provided at establishment" one directory over, which makes the README's claim read as deliberate.
- **claim:** §4.1-§4.10 — one continuous lifecycle in which each stage is actually composed, verified, tested and validated before the VSB is established and output.
- **observed:** _resolve_body_fields (agentic_core/api/genesis.py:971) computes `is_floor = bool(text.strip()) and ...`, so an EMPTY body field is recorded as pending=False, i.e. "not awaiting the owned model". _derived_stage (agentic_core/api/vsb.py:1660) then walks body_pending, finds no gap, and emits the "(every §4 section on this entity is composed)" clause. The function's own docstring was written to kill exactly this defect ("the entity said in one field that it had commercialised and in the next that it had not composed its concept") — it fixed the pending case and left the empty case, which now reads as the strongest possible claim. The journey path is honest by contrast: the same template printed "Stage: no stage reached · Status: held · no §4 lifecycle stage is reached: the first section (concept) still awaits the owned model" for vsb-9313233561.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/establish {problem, domain:agriculture, realm:physical, name:"TamrCool Cooperative"} (no concept/design/commercialisation) -> vsb-a42ca383af, body_pending {concept:false, design:false, commercialisation:false, operations:false}, stage "commercialisation". GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-a42ca383af -> genesis_blueprint {"concept":"","design":"","commercialisation":""}, stage_basis "...(every §4 section on this entity is composed)". C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_repos/vsb-a42ca383af/README.md lines 5-7; BUSINESS_PLAN.md "## Concept" followed by a blank line. Renderers: agentic_core/api/vsb.py:117 (README), VSBCockpit.tsx:676-681 (badge + stage_basis), vsb.py:1362 (Board Pack operational layer).
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the README states the inverse of the truth, and the Cockpit repeats it verbatim.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). CONFIRMED exactly as described, tier 1 held. The bug is one `bool(text.strip()) and` in genesis.py:969 that collapses 'this field is empty' into 'this field is not awaiting the owned model', and _derived_stage — a function written specifically to kill the inverse of this defect, per its own docstring — then reads that as composition. Tier 1 rather than 2: the reader is shown the sentence '(every §4 section on this entity is composed)' in the shipped README and on the Cockpit badge while IDENTITY.md one directory over shows three empty strings and BUSINESS_PLAN.md shows three empty headings. That is an affirmative false statement about the entity's own state, so the 'an omission is not an assertion' carve-out does not reach it. Tier 1 rather than 3 because nothing qualifies it — and the qualification exists in the same repository (OPERATIONS.md and EVIDENCE.md both print 'Not provided at establishment'), which is what makes the README's claim read as deliberate rather than as a gap. Verdict STUB is right: the stage field and its basis are a surface that pretends.
- **refuter's evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/establish {problem, domain:agriculture, realm:physical, name:'RefuteCool Cooperative'} with no concept/design/commercialisation -> vsb-cdc8805381, body_pending {concept:false, design:false, commercialisation:false, operations:false}. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-cdc8805381 -> stage 'commercialisation', stage_basis 'the furthest §4 section composed with no gap before it: commercialisation (every §4 section on this entity is composed)', genesis_blueprint {'concept':'','design':'','commercialisation':''}. Shipped C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_repos/vsb-cdc8805381/README.md line 5-7: '**Stage:** commercialisation · **Status:** registered - not operating' / '**Stage basis:** ... (every §4 section on this entity is composed)'. BUSINESS_PLAN.md: '## Concept' followed by a blank line, '## Design & Development' blank, '## Commercialisation' blank. Mechanism: agentic_core/api/genesis.py:969 `is_floor = bool(text.strip()) and (...)` -> an empty field yields pending=False; agentic_core/api/vsb.py:1659-1695 _derived_stage then finds no gap and emits the all-composed clause. Contrast in the same install: the journey-established vsb-5f26b5c0c4 (bodies genuinely pending) honestly reports body_pending all true and status 'held'. Renderer: VSBCockpit.tsx:676.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** In _resolve_body_fields (agentic_core/api/genesis.py:971) record an empty body field as pending too (`pending[field] = True` when `not text.strip()`), so _derived_stage sees the gap; and make the "every §4 section is composed" clause in agentic_core/api/vsb.py:1700 require non-empty text rather than merely `pend[k] is False`.

### R2.2 · The VSB Spawn Studio tells the user "VSB is now operational" one line after the server saved its status as "body pending" — **STUB** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The terminal SSE event is `{"stage":"complete","label":"VSB Operational","content":"VSB vsb-4620d8faab is now operational."}`, and VSBSpawnStudio.tsx:317/320 renders `{ev.label}` and `{ev.content}` straight into the page with a Sparkles icon in the accent colour. Six lines earlier in the same generator the server computed `_derived_status(vsb_entity)` -> ("body pending", "1 body section(s) still await the owned model: ceo_specification") and persisted it. The user is shown "operational"; the record says otherwise. That is a positive false claim on a page a person uses, not a disclosed gap — the honest string already exists in the same function and is simply not the one emitted.
- **claim:** §4.8/§4.10 — the established VSB self-operates and forever runs, defends, heals, learns, improves and grows; §15.6 honesty over polish, never fabricate.
- **observed:** agentic_core/api/vsb.py:2369 `yield _event("complete", "VSB Operational", f"VSB {vsb_id} is now operational.", {...})`. The entity it refers to has ceo_specification = "content pending the owned model — this enterprise has not yet composed its own CEO specification", status "body pending", stage null, and the living roster reports autonomous_cycles false for every entity, so nothing tends it. The rest of this same stream is scrupulous (it says the six cognitive engines "return fixed markers rather than reading the challenge" and that the constitutional gate was "NOT evaluated"), which makes the final line the one unguarded claim.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/vsb/spawn {challenge:"Cold-chain loss for 40 date farms in Medina...",name:"SpawnTamr",scope:"commercialise"} -> last SSE frame `label "VSB Operational", content "VSB vsb-4620d8faab is now operational.", elapsed_seconds 0.81`. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-4620d8faab -> status "body pending", status_basis "1 body section(s) still await the owned model: ceo_specification", stage null. Renderer: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/VSBSpawnStudio.tsx:317,320; route registered at App.tsx:189-190 (/vsb and /vsb-spawn).
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the stream's own final word is "operational".
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). CONFIRMED by execution, tier 1 held. The honest string is computed, assigned to vsb_entity['status'] and written to disk SIX LINES ABOVE the event that contradicts it, in the same generator. Tier 1 rather than 2: 'VSB vsb-3f7557af27 is now operational.' is a positive assertion of an outcome the platform's own persisted record denies in the same request; it is not a blank field and not a hedged one. Tier 1 rather than 3 because the stream does not state this shortfall anywhere — and the contrast is damning, since the same stream candidly labels its own frames 'Six Cognitive Engines (fixed responses)', 'Cascade Complete (fixed markers)' and 'CEO Strategy Pending'. The one line that is not candid is the last thing the user reads. Verdict STUB: the terminal frame is a surface that pretends an operating enterprise exists 1.27 seconds after a form submit.
- **refuter's evidence:** POST /api/v1/vsb/spawn {challenge:'Cold-chain loss for 40 date farms in Medina...', name:'RefuteSpawn', scope:'commercialise'} -> 15 SSE frames, terminal frame verbatim: data: {"stage": "complete", "label": "VSB Operational", "content": "VSB vsb-3f7557af27 is now operational.", "data": {... "elapsed_seconds": 1.27}}. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-3f7557af27 -> status 'body pending', status_basis '1 body section(s) still await the owned model: ceo_specification', stage null, stage_basis 'the body-pending map names no §4 lifecycle section (it holds ceo_specification), so the stage reached is NOT KNOWN'. GET /api/v1/economy/living-vsbs -> autonomous_cycles false, autonomous_cycles_note 'OFF — the heartbeat's Self-run lever is off'. Code: agentic_core/api/vsb.py:2359-2364 computes and PERSISTS _derived_status(vsb_entity) -> ('body pending', ...), then vsb.py:2371 yields the 'VSB Operational' / 'is now operational.' event. Renderer: VSBSpawnStudio.tsx:317 {ev.label} and :320 {ev.content}, route /vsb and /vsb-spawn (App.tsx:189-190).

### R2.3 · The Organism page's green "operational" count and readiness percentage are computed from two status words no writer produces any more — **PARTIAL** · tier 1

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** OrganismAnatomy.tsx:278 renders `<Chip tone="ok">1 operational</Chip>` and :280 renders "Readiness 8%" with the basis "0.5 x VSB operational share (1/6)". No entity is operating: the living roster reports autonomous_cycles false, and the six stored entities hold statuses "body pending" x3, "held", "registered - not operating", and one malformed legacy record whose status literal is "active" and whose vsb_id and name are both null. The counter `operational = sum(n for s, n in by_status.items() if s in ("operational", "active"))` matches only the vocabulary W496 abolished, so it counts the broken record and can NEVER count the status _derived_status actually returns ("operating"). A reader is shown a green count of 1 and a quantified readiness figure derived from it; both are untrue, and the number is displayed, not merely stored — so tier 1 rather than tier 2.
- **claim:** §4.10 — Run · Defend · Heal · Learn · Improve · Grow, forever; §15.6 never fabricate a figure.
- **observed:** agentic_core/api/organism_status.py:123 fixes the predicate to ("operational","active"). The comment above it records the previous half of this same bug ("W438 — the only status the VSB writer persists is 'operational'; this counted 'active'") — then W496 replaced the literal statuses with derived ones ("operating", "body pending", "held", "registered - not operating") and this reader was not moved with them. The same `operational` value feeds `vsb_operational: operational > 0` (:487) and `commercialisation_readiness` (:476), so one stale vocabulary drives a boolean, a count and a percentage.
- **evidence:** GET /api/v1/organism/lifecycle -> `"vsb_operational": true, "vsb_entities": {"total": 6, "operational": 1, "by_status": {"active": 1, "body pending": 3, "held": 1, "registered - not operating": 1}}, "commercialisation_readiness": 0.0833, basis "0.5 x project commercialisation_rate (0.0) + 0.5 x VSB operational share (1/6)"`. The one "active" record is C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_entities/vsb-0a6ca2ab.json with vsb_id null and name null. GET /api/v1/economy/living-vsbs -> every row operating_cycles 0, last_operated null. Renderer: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/organism/OrganismAnatomy.tsx:79,278,280.
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the chip carries the 'ok' (green) tone and the basis line names a share that is itself wrong.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). CONFIRMED by execution, tier 1 held — and one factual correction that makes it WORSE, not better. The assessor called the counted record 'a malformed legacy record'. It is not legacy: it was written today at 14:50 by agentic_core/api/synthesis_studio.py:320, which is a live, routed establishment writer that stores `status: "active"` as a literal and never calls _derived_status. So the organism's green 'operational' chip is not counting a stale artefact, it is structurally counting exactly and only the entities born down the one path that bypasses the derivation — and it can never count the status the derivation actually returns ('operating'), because 'operating' is not in the predicate's tuple. Tier 1 rather than 2: OrganismAnatomy.tsx:278 renders a green chip reading '1 operational' and :280 a quantified 'Readiness 6%' whose own basis sentence names 'VSB operational share (1/8)'. No entity is operating — autonomous_cycles is false, so by the platform's own definition nothing tends any of them. A displayed count and a displayed percentage are both untrue, and a fabricated figure is named in the tier-1 definition. Tier 1 rather than 3 because nothing on the card qualifies the number; the basis line beside it repeats the wrong share rather than correcting it. Verdict PARTIAL is right (the lifecycle route is real and mostly honest; one counter's vocabulary is stale).
- **refuter's evidence:** GET /api/v1/organism/lifecycle (live, twice) -> first: vsb_operational true, vsb_entities {total:6, operational:1, by_status:{active:1, 'body pending':3, held:1, 'registered - not operating':1}}, commercialisation_readiness 0.0833, basis '0.5 x project commercialisation_rate (0.0) + 0.5 x VSB operational share (1/6)'. After my three establishments: {total:8, operational:1, by_status:{active:1, 'body pending':4, held:1, 'registered - not operating':2}}, readiness 0.0625, basis '... (1/8)'. Counter: agentic_core/api/organism_status.py:123 `operational = sum(n for s, n in by_status.items() if s in ('operational','active'))`. Every return of _derived_status (agentic_core/api/vsb.py:1634,1638,1644,1647,1650) is one of 'held', 'body pending', 'registered - not operating', 'registered - operation unknown', 'operating' — 'operational' and 'active' are unreachable from it. The single counted record is C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_entities/vsb-0a6ca2ab.json: status 'active', vsb_id null, name null, created_at 2026-10-05T14:50:24Z, keyed on entity_id. Its writer is LIVE: agentic_core/api/synthesis_studio.py:320 `"status": "active"` (also :409, and capital_fund.py:285). GET /api/v1/economy/living-vsbs -> autonomous_cycles false. Renderers: OrganismAnatomy.tsx:278 <Chip tone={life.vsb_operational ? 'ok' : 'dim'}>{life.vsb_entities.operational} operational</Chip>, :280 'Readiness {pct(...)}' plus the basis line at :281.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** At agentic_core/api/organism_status.py:123 count the vocabulary _derived_status returns ("operating"), report any legacy "operational"/"active" rows as a separate named bucket, and name in the basis string which statuses were counted.

### R2.4 · The spawn stream says the Board is chaired by the owner's Chief twin, while every other surface says no twin model is trained — **PARTIAL** *(assessed STUB)* · tier 2 *(assessed tier 1)*

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The governance event reads "Board chaired by the owner's Chief twin; waqf_ltd_hybrid economy initialised; registered as a living entity; business plan seeded." and is rendered verbatim by VSBSpawnStudio.tsx:320. The entity record written by the same call says the opposite: board.chief.title = "Chief of the Board — the founder's standing charter, instructions and recorded decisions on the owned fabric (no twin model is trained...)" and native_swarm.org[0] = "Chief (the Owner's standing charter; no twin model is trained)". The code comment three lines above the event string even says "the Owner's standing charter, not a trained twin — W492/FU-188". So the page asserts a digital twin that the platform states, elsewhere in the same payload, it does not have. An assertion of a capability that does not exist is tier 1, not tier 3; nothing on the page qualifies it.
- **claim:** §15.3 — Chief = the founder's digital twin, apex, arms-length above the AI CEO, faithful in presence and absence.
- **observed:** agentic_core/api/vsb.py:2350-2352 emits the "Chief twin" wording; vsb.py:2345-2347 is the comment disclaiming a trained twin. The genesis/establish path and the entity's own board/native_swarm records all carry the honest "standing charter, no twin model is trained" sentence. Two surfaces of the same system disagree, and the one a user watches live makes the stronger claim.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/vsb/spawn -> SSE frame `{"stage":"governance","label":"Board + Living Economy Attached","content":"Board chaired by the owner's Chief twin; ..."}`. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-a42ca383af -> board.chief.title "...(no twin model is trained...)"; native_swarm.org[0] "Chief (the Owner's standing charter; no twin model is trained)". Renderer: VSBSpawnStudio.tsx:320.
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the live stream is where the twin claim is made and it is not qualified there.
- **refutation: REFUTED — corrected to PARTIAL.** REFUTED as to verdict and tier; the underlying gap is real but was tiered one step too harsh. The word 'twin' is the VISION'S OWN NAME for the role — §5 and §15.3 both define 'Chief = the Founder's Digital Twin' — so a frame saying 'Board chaired by the owner's Chief twin' is using the platform's chartered role name, not minting a capability claim. And a Board IS attached: board.chief exists, the economy initialised, the entity registered. No surface anywhere asserts a twin MODEL was trained; the authoritative record the same frame links to (dashboard: /api/v1/vsb/{id}) states the opposite explicitly. So this is not 'an assertion of a capability that does not exist' — it is one live line that drops a qualifier the record carries. That lands on tier 2, not tier 1: it is partial (the role is a standing charter, not a modelled twin) and this particular surface does not say so. It is not tier 3 because the Spawn Studio page, which discloses its engine weakness in two separate paragraphs, says nothing at all about the Chief being a charter rather than a model, so a reader on that page is not told. Verdict corrected STUB -> PARTIAL: a frame that uses the vision's role vocabulary without its qualifier is an under-disclosure, not a pretence.
- **refuter's evidence:** POST /api/v1/vsb/spawn -> governance frame verbatim: data: {"stage": "governance", "label": "Board + Living Economy Attached", "content": "Board chaired by the owner's Chief twin; waqf_ltd_hybrid economy initialised; registered as a living entity; business plan seeded."}. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-3f7557af27 -> board.chief.title "Chief of the Board — the founder's standing charter, instructions and recorded decisions on the owned fabric (no twin model is trained; the Chief reports whether it is a modelled twin or a role)". (native_swarm.org[0] came back None in my run, so that half of the assessor's evidence did not reproduce.) Code: agentic_core/api/vsb.py:2348-2350 comment "the Owner's standing charter, not a trained twin — W492/FU-188" immediately above the event at :2351-2352. THE VISION ITSELF: docs/WORKSTATION_IDBO_WHOLE_VISION.md §15.3 "**Chief = the founder's digital twin**, apex, arms-length above the AI CEO" and §5 "**Chief of the Board of Directors = the Founder's Digital Twin.**" Renderer: VSBSpawnStudio.tsx:320.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Replace "the owner's Chief twin" at agentic_core/api/vsb.py:2351 with the sentence the entity's own board record already carries: "the Owner's standing charter (no twin model is trained)".

### R2.5 · EVIDENCE.md heads a section "§4.5 evidence-ranked" and prints "simulated evidence: 0.8" for a run in which nothing was simulated and no comparison happened — **PARTIAL** · tier 2

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The shipped EVIDENCE.md opens "## Selected Candidate (§4.5 evidence-ranked)" and the fourth bullet reads "- simulated evidence: 0.8 (modelled 0.862; declared weights 60/40)". Both are assertions of outcomes the platform did not produce: the digital-twin stage was floor-served, all three candidates returned byte-identical text (candidates_distinct=1), and the journey's own ranking was a tie resolved by list order. The 0.8 is `_score_candidate` run over the floor's term-frequency text — a shape proxy relabelled "simulated evidence". It is tier 2 and not tier 1 because the correction does exist in the same file, two bullets down under a different heading ("simulation pending the owned model ... a structured frame, not a simulation" and "comparison: TIE ... no comparison happened"); a reader scanning the Selected Candidate bullets gets the figure without it, so the shortfall is real but not wholly undisclosed. It is not tier 3 because the heading and the figure make positive claims rather than stating the shortfall.
- **claim:** §4.5 — every candidate solution is specifically designed, modelled, simulated, optimised, categorised and ranked so the best is selected on evidence.
- **observed:** The attestation layer at agentic_core/api/genesis.py:210-224 does this correctly: it WITHHOLDS "simulated" ("the twin returned one identical output for all 3 candidates — nothing was simulated apart") and "ranked"/"optimised" on a tie. The EVIDENCE.md writer at agentic_core/api/vsb.py:162-164 does not consult that withheld map — it prints simulation_score whenever the field is non-null. So the QMS record is honest and the founder-facing file is not.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/journey {establish:true, review_gates:[design]} -> stage_5_model_simulate_rank.candidates_distinct 1, candidates_are_alternatives false, tie.detected true, tie.tiebreak_is_merit false, candidate[0].simulation_score 0.8, modelled_score 0.862. C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_repos/vsb-9313233561/EVIDENCE.md lines 3-9.
- **disclosed to the user:** Partly — the contradiction is present in the same file under a later sub-heading, but the heading and the figure are not themselves qualified.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). CONFIRMED, tier 2 held. The split is exactly as described: the attestation builder has a correct withhold-with-reason mechanism for 'simulated', 'modelled', 'ranked' and 'optimised', and the founder-facing EVIDENCE.md writer does not consult it — it gates on the figure's non-nullity alone. Tier 2 rather than 1: the heading '(§4.5 evidence-ranked)' and the bullet 'simulated evidence: 0.8' are both assertions of outcomes the platform did not produce (0.793/0.8 is _score_candidate run over the floor's own term-frequency text, relabelled 'simulated evidence'), but the contradiction is printed in the SAME FILE two bullets down under '### Simulation excerpt', where 'no comparison happened' appears in those words. A reader who reads the file through is corrected; a reader who scans the Selected Candidate bullets is not. Tier 2 rather than 3 because the heading and the figure make positive claims rather than stating the shortfall — the correction is adjacent, not attached to the claim it corrects.
- **refuter's evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/journey {problem (musharakah cold-store), establish:true, review_gates:['design']} -> 47487 bytes in 1.88 s. stage_5_model_simulate_rank: candidates_distinct 1, candidates_are_alternatives false, tie {detected:true, tied_candidates:[pragmatic,innovative,lean], tiebreak_is_merit:false, resolved_by:'the declared tiebreak: the first candidate in the fixed framing order ... — NOT evidence'}, comparison_note 'All 3 candidates returned IDENTICAL text, so no comparison was possible and the ranking below carries no information.', candidate[0].simulation_score 0.793, modelled_score 0.798. Shipped C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_repos/vsb-9313233561/EVIDENCE.md lines 3-9 read verbatim: '## Selected Candidate (§4.5 evidence-ranked)' ... '- simulated evidence: 0.8 (modelled 0.862; declared weights 60/40)' then '### Simulation excerpt' / 'simulation pending the owned model — the deterministic floor served the digital-twin stage; its output is a structured frame, not a simulation' / '- comparison: TIE ... candidates_distinct=1 — the ranked alternatives were identical text; no comparison happened'. The honest layer: agentic_core/api/genesis.py:208-222 WITHHOLDS 'simulated' with reason 'the twin returned one identical output for all 3 candidates — nothing was simulated apart' and withholds 'ranked'/'optimised' on a tie. The file writer: agentic_core/api/vsb.py:161-164 prints simulation_score on the sole condition `if _cand.get('simulation_score') is not None` and never reads the withheld map.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Gate the "(§4.5 evidence-ranked)" heading and the "simulated evidence" bullet at agentic_core/api/vsb.py:158-164 on the same evidence/withheld map genesis.py:210-224 already computes — print "form only; no simulation and no comparison" when `simulated` or `ranked` is withheld.

### R2.6 · The three §13 surfaces are called "integrated" but have zero cross-links, and the Web app and Phone app ship byte-identical code — **PARTIAL** · tier 3 *(assessed tier 2)*

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The shipped README states "`web/`, `webapp/`, `mobile/` — integrated Website / Web app / Phone app", and the ship manifest reports `coherent_whole: true` under a route whose docstring says "ship the entity repository as ONE COHERENT WHOLE ... not four disconnected calls". Measured: no page in web/ links to webapp/ or mobile/ at all (every href is index/about/solution/styles.css), webapp/index.html links only its own stylesheet, and webapp/app.js and mobile/app.js plus webapp/data.json and mobile/data.json are byte-identical — the "Web app" and the "Phone app" are one application shipped twice. Tier 2 rather than tier 1 because "integrated" is ambiguous enough to be read as "integrated into this repository", and because `coherent_whole` is honestly documented elsewhere as per-surface generator success; tier 2 rather than 3 because nothing anywhere tells the founder the surfaces do not reference each other or that two of the three are duplicates — the `features` lists for webapp and mobile are presented as separate capability sets.
- **claim:** §13 — the canonical output integrates a Website, a Web app and a Phone (mobile) app as the entity's public + operational surfaces, shipped as a coherent, version-controlled whole.
- **observed:** generate_vsb_website, generate_vsb_webapp and generate_vsb_mobile each write their own directory from the same entity data with no reference to one another. `coherent_whole` at agentic_core/api/vsb.py:2437 is `all("error" not in s and "deferred" not in s for s in surfaces.values())` — it measures that four generators did not raise, which is a different proposition from the §13 integration claim the README then makes. The individual notes are otherwise exemplary ("Static info site — NOT a running web app", "NOT a compiled native iOS/Android app, not deployed/hosted").
- **evidence:** C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_repos/vsb-9313233561: `grep -o 'href="[^"]*"' web/*.html` -> only about.html, index.html, solution.html, styles.css; `grep -o 'href="[^"]*"' webapp/index.html` -> styles.css only; `diff webapp/data.json mobile/data.json` -> identical; `diff webapp/app.js mobile/app.js` -> identical. GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-9313233561/repo -> integrated_surfaces all "(generated)"; establish initial_ship -> coherent_whole true. README.md structure list, final bullet.
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the word used is "integrated", and the duplication of webapp/mobile is not mentioned on any surface.
- **refutation: REFUTED — corrected to PARTIAL.** REFUTED in its headline and corrected down to tier 3. The duplication charge does not survive measurement: webapp and mobile share app.js and data.json but their shells differ, and mobile uniquely ships a web manifest, a service worker and an icon — which is precisely the distinction §13's own W446 clause draws between 'a real client-side interactive app' and 'a real installable PWA'. Sharing one data-driven app core between a browser build and a PWA build is ordinary engineering reuse, not 'one application shipped twice', and the two features lists name the real differences (installable, offline) rather than inflating them. So 'ship byte-identical code' and 'two of the three are duplicates' are not supported. What survives is only the cross-link observation, and §13's word 'integrated' most naturally reads — and the README explicitly frames it — as integrated into the entity's repository as its surfaces, which is what is on disk. Tier 3 rather than 2: each surface's own note states its scope candidly and unprompted ('Static info site — NOT a running web app', 'NOT a compiled native iOS/Android app, not deployed/hosted'), and the README points the reader at integrated_surfaces for the real state, so a reader is told what they are getting. The genuinely undisclosed contradiction in these directories is NOT this one: webapp/README.md and mobile/README.md shipped on disk still read '(scaffold) ... built in a later increment (not a compiled app)' sitting beside the real generated app files, because the ship ordering left those placeholders in place — that is the same root cause as index 0 and belongs there, and it under-claims rather than over-claims.
- **refuter's evidence:** C:/tmp/m1data2/data/vsb_repos/vsb-cdc8805381. Cross-links (the half that STANDS): `grep -oh 'href="[^"]*"' web/*.html | sort -u` -> about.html, index.html, solution.html, styles.css only; webapp/index.html -> styles.css only; mobile/index.html -> icon.svg, manifest.webmanifest, styles.css only. No page in any surface references another surface. Duplication (the half that FAILS): `diff webapp/X mobile/X` -> app.js IDENTICAL (2596 b), data.json IDENTICAL (3427 b), but index.html DIFFERS (293 vs 588 b) and styles.css DIFFERS (1060 vs 1109 b); mobile additionally ships manifest.webmanifest (535 b), sw.js (541 b) and icon.svg (255 b) which webapp does not. mobile/app.json note: 'Bespoke installable PWA (real web manifest + service worker + icon + mobile-first client-side app). Installable + offline-capable when hosted. NOT a compiled native iOS/Android app, not deployed/hosted.' features: ['installable (web manifest + icon)','offline (service worker cache)','mobile-first interactive app (tabs + live filter)']. webapp/app.json note: 'Bespoke client-side interactive web app (vanilla HTML/CSS/JS, data-driven from the entity, runs directly in a browser — no build). NOT a server/backend app, not deployed/hosted.' web/site.json note: 'Static info site — NOT a running web app (increment 3), not deployed/hosted.' Vision §13 W446 clause: 'the **Website** is a real multi-page static site, the **Web app** a real client-side interactive app, the **Phone app** a real installable PWA (offline-capable when hosted)'. README final structure bullet: '`web/`, `webapp/`, `mobile/` — integrated Website / Web app / Phone app (see `integrated_surfaces` in `manifest.json` for which are generated and which are still scaffolds; none is a built or running app)'.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Compute an `integration` field from real cross-references (does web/ link to webapp/ or mobile/; do webapp and mobile differ) and have the README bullet and `coherent_whole` report what it measured — and either cross-link the three surfaces or say they are independent copies.

### R2.7 · There is not ONE gated lifecycle: two routed pages establish VSBs and only Genesis runs §4 or honours the review gates — **PARTIAL** · tier 2

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** /vsb and /vsb-spawn both route to VSB Spawn Studio (App.tsx:189-190), whose header says "Spawn a Virtual Sovereign Business from a challenge. Runs the pipeline: Six Cognitive Engines → MJM → GaaS → CEO Strategy → Genome" and which offers a Scope selector including "commercialise". Entities it creates carry genesis_blueprint null, stage null, no candidates, no stage verifications, no review-gate config — none of §4.2-§4.9 runs, and the Mode-3 gates that genuinely block the Genesis path cannot apply because no gate is ever configured here. The page is unusually candid about its engines (it says they return fixed markers) but says nothing about skipping the §4 lifecycle, and offering "commercialise" as a scope invites the opposite conclusion. Tier 2: the shortfall is large and real and the surface does not state it; not tier 1 because no single sentence on the page is false about §4 — it simply never mentions it.
- **claim:** §4 — ONE continuous, intelligent, autonomous workflow that takes a plainly-described challenge all the way to a living enterprise, each stage modelled, simulated, optimised, ranked, verified, tested and validated.
- **observed:** Three establishment entry points exist: POST /api/v1/genesis/journey (runs §4.2-§4.9, honours Mode-3 gates), POST /api/v1/genesis/establish (same store, bodies optional — see the empty-body finding), and POST /api/v1/vsb/spawn (no §4 at all, 0.81 s end to end). /api/v1/studio/vsb/spawn is a fourth, requiring project_id + solution. Only the first is the gated lifecycle the vision describes.
- **evidence:** apps/workstation-superapp/src/App.tsx:189-191,193. POST /api/v1/vsb/spawn -> GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-4620d8faab shows genesis_blueprint null, stage null, body_pending {ceo_specification:true}, scope "commercialise", review_gates absent. By contrast the Genesis-established vsb-9313233561 carries review_gates {stages:[design]} and its gate refused six movers with HTTP 409. VSBSpawnStudio.tsx:202-204 (header pipeline sentence), :257 (Scope selector).
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the Spawn Studio discloses its engine weakness in detail but never says this path bypasses the §4 lifecycle and its gates.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). CONFIRMED, tier 2 held, and I verified the fourth entry point the assessor only named: synthesis_studio.py:375 is routed, reachable, and writes a VSB record with a literal status that no derivation produces — it is the same writer whose output drives the false 'operational' count in index 3, so §4's fragmentation and that fabricated figure share one root. Tier 2 rather than 1: I read the Spawn Studio page source for a false sentence and there is none — the header says 'Runs the pipeline: Six Cognitive Engines → MJM → GaaS → CEO Strategy → Genome', and that pipeline genuinely runs; the page simply never mentions §4, its stages, or its gates. Offering a 'commercialise' scope on a path where no §4 stage executes and no gate can ever be configured invites the wrong inference, but it is an inference, not a statement. Tier 2 rather than 3 because nothing on the page discloses that this route bypasses the lifecycle — and the page's unusual candour about its engines makes a reader more, not less, likely to trust what it does not qualify.
- **refuter's evidence:** Four live establishment writers, all into the same data/vsb_entities store: (1) POST /api/v1/genesis/journey — ran §4.2-§4.9, carried review_gates {stages:['design']}, and its gate refused six movers with 409; (2) POST /api/v1/genesis/establish — same store, bodies optional (see index 1); (3) POST /api/v1/vsb/spawn — GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-3f7557af27 shows genesis_blueprint null, stage null, review_gates null, body_pending {ceo_specification:true}, scope 'commercialise', 1.27 s end to end; (4) POST /api/v1/studio/vsb/spawn (agentic_core/api/synthesis_studio.py:375) — writes entity_id (not vsb_id), status 'active' as a literal, no body_pending, no gates; its record vsb-0a6ca2ab is readable at GET /api/v1/vsb/vsb-0a6ca2ab -> 200. Routing: App.tsx:189 '/vsb' and :190 '/vsb-spawn' both -> VSBSpawnStudio; :193 '/genesis' -> GenesisJourney. VSBSpawnStudio.tsx:201-203 header: 'Spawn a Virtual Sovereign Business from a challenge. Runs the pipeline: Six Cognitive Engines → MJM → GaaS → CEO Strategy → Genome.' Scope selector at :257 includes 'commercialise'. Vision §4: 'One **continuous, intelligent, autonomous workflow** ... that takes a plainly-described challenge all the way to a living enterprise.'
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Add one line to the VSBSpawnStudio header stating that this path does not run the §4 Concept→Commercialisation stages or the Mode-3 review gates, with a link to /genesis — or route /vsb and /vsb-spawn into the Genesis lifecycle.

### R2.8 · Every lifecycle stage delivers the user's own words plus a term-frequency list, not solution work — and says so at every surface — **PARTIAL** · tier 3

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Seven of seven stage bodies are the engine's scaffold. The Design stage's "Solution Architecture" for a solar cold-store cooperative reads "Core capability: address inconsistent cold-chain. · Component for inconsistent cold-chain. · Component for affordable solar-powered. · Interfaces & data: specify inputs/outputs and contracts." — the user's own bigrams wrapped in generic bullets; nothing about refrigeration, solar sizing, musharakah or 40 farms. But the shortfall is stated at every surface a person reaches, in the surface's own words: each block is prefixed "The list below is the request's most frequent terms, not findings about them" or "Structured architecture frame" / "deterministic scaffold"; stages_note says "7 of 7 stages were served by the deterministic floor and are not assessable"; every stage verification returns `verified: null` with a basis explaining the proxies cannot fail; the Genesis page renders an amber card above the results reading "The deterministic native floor served 15 of these calls — honest structured composition from your inputs, not generative model reasoning"; the shipped Business Plan says "content pending the owned model"; and the public website carries that same sentence. A shortfall the surface itself states is tier 3 however large, and "no model ran" is the environment (AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=1), not a defect — so this is tier 3, not tier 2.
- **claim:** §4.2-§4.7 — understand the real underlying problem; discover the best, latest and most advanced approaches across science, technology, business, operations and law; produce a buildable design; apply operational intelligence.
- **observed:** The only two stages that read the user's problem rather than counting its words are the §11 compliance screen (which returned sharia_halal: review, uk_legal: review on the halal profit-sharing text, and honestly recorded three frameworks as not_assessed) and the derived-stage selector, which fired `risk` on the literal "halal" and `economics` on "profit" and names that basis. The whole journey completed in 0.78 s. One real quality defect inside the floor: a section heading is emitted truncated mid-word — "## Problem Understandi" — immediately followed by the correct "## Problem Understanding", so the delivered text carries a mangled duplicate heading, and "## Key Steps" is emitted with three blank lines as its body.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/journey (0.78 s, 46920 bytes) -> stages_floor_served 7, stages_verified "0/0", ai_provenance.served_by {native: 15}, any_external false. phase_2_design_development text as quoted. stage_5 comparison_note "All 3 candidates returned IDENTICAL text, so no comparison was possible and the ranking below carries no information." Disclosure renderers: GenesisJourney.tsx:708-730 (provenance card), :737-755 (three-state verification chips, slate dash for not-assessable). Truncated heading: stage_5_model_simulate_rank.candidates[0].approach.
- **disclosed to the user:** Yes — thoroughly, at the API, in the UI above the results, in the shipped repo and on the generated public website.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). CONFIRMED on substance, disclosure and tier 3 — with one sub-claim struck. The assessor reported a heading emitted truncated mid-word ('## Problem Understandi' followed by the correct heading). I enumerated every line starting with '#' in the floor output and the headings are clean: ## SOCH, ## AQAL, ## Problem Understanding, ## Approach, ## Key Steps, ## Effectiveness, ## Risks & Mitigations. What DOES reproduce is different and milder: the problem text is truncated mid-word inside bodies ('... financed by halal profi'), and '## Key Steps' is emitted with three blank lines as its body. Both are omissions or cosmetic defects, not assertions, so neither lifts the tier. Tier 3 and not tier 2 because the shortfall is stated at every surface a person reaches, in that surface's own words — the per-block prefixes, stages_note, every stage verification returning verified:null with a basis saying the proxies cannot fail, the amber card ABOVE the results, the shipped plan and the generated public site — and the tier rule is explicit that a shortfall the surface itself states is tier 3 however large. Tier 3 and not 0 because the vision's §4.2-§4.7 promise discovery across science, technology, business, operations and law, and a bigram count is not that: this is the single widest capability gap in R2, and it is honestly labelled.
- **refuter's evidence:** POST /api/v1/genesis/journey (1.88 s, 47487 bytes) -> stages_floor_served 7, stages_verified '0/0', stages_note '7 of 7 stages were served by the deterministic floor and are not assessable — the verification proxies cannot fail on floor output, so no verdict is claimed for them', ai_provenance.served_by {native:15}, any_external false. phase_2_design_development verbatim: '## Solution Architecture\nStructured architecture frame for: ... - Core capability: address affordable solar-powered. - Component for affordable solar-powered. - Component for inconsistent cold-chain. - Component for harvest inconsistent. - Component for solar-powered cold. - Interfaces & data: specify inputs/outputs and contracts.' Every term list is prefixed 'The list below is the request's most frequent terms, not findings about them.' or 'Structured frame over the terms most frequent in the request (a term count, not an analysis)'. UI disclosure verified in source: apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/synthesis/GenesisJourney.tsx:721-724 renders, in amber, above the results: 'The deterministic native floor served {n} of these calls — honest structured composition from your inputs, not generative model reasoning. Run a local model (see /native-ai) for generative output.'; :743-747 renders '0/0 verified · 7 floor-served, not assessable' in slate (the emerald branch requires m>0 && n===m); :750-755 renders a slate em-dash chip per stage with the basis in title. Shipped BUSINESS_PLAN.md and web/ carry 'content pending the owned model'.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Nothing on the truth axis; the capability needs the owned model (a local model with AI_DISABLE_LOCAL=0). Separately, fix the floor's heading truncation so a delivered section heading is never cut mid-word.

### R2.9 · DELIVERED: the Mode-3 review gate really stops the lifecycle, and the repository is a real version-controlled, downloadable whole — **DELIVERED**

- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Verdict DELIVERED, so no tier. Verified by execution rather than by reading: a pending `design` gate refused six separate movers with HTTP 409 and a per-mover reason, the establish-time ship refused itself (`shipped: false, deferred: "review gate"`), and after one approve call all four generators returned 200. This is the §17.4 Mode-3 gate working as the vision describes, and it is the single strongest thing in R2.
- **claim:** §4 — one gated lifecycle with optional human review at any Concept→Commercialisation stage; §13 — shipped as a coherent, version-controlled whole.
- **observed:** With gate design pending: POST /repo, /repo/ship, /website, /webapp, /mobile and /board-pack each returned 409 naming the mover ("repo refused", "ship refused", "website refused", "web app refused", "phone app refused", "board pack refused") with blocking [{stage: design, status: pending}]. The gate decision is DCS-audited (a 128-hex dcs_hash). After approval the repo grew to 27 files / 76111 bytes with four real git commits, GET /repo/zip returned a 29661-byte application/zip with all 27 entries, GET /repo/file?path=README.md returned the file with an x-repo-bytes header, and a `../../../../etc/passwd` path returned 404 rather than traversing. §4.10 is gated too: POST /evolve filed one evidence-based proposal as cca-3c50ddef98 with `applied: false`, `generation: 0` and the basis "the genome mutates only on POST .../evolution/apply after the Owner approves" — self-improvement that proposes rather than asserts. /api/v1/deliverables/output-formats separates eight live renders from six `catalogue_not_yet_produced` targets and labels its video as "Self-playing video (animated HTML — auto-advancing scenes)" rather than implying mp4.
- **evidence:** POST /api/v1/vsb/vsb-9313233561/{repo,repo/ship,website,webapp,mobile,board-pack} -> 409 x6 while gate pending. POST /review-gates/design/decision {approve} -> 200, status approved, blocks_progress false, dcs_hash b886139a5b6b... Then website/webapp/mobile/repo -> 200 x4. GET /repo/zip -> 200, 29661 bytes, 27 entries. GET /repo/file?path=../../../../etc/passwd -> 404. POST /evolve -> outcome "proposals_filed_pending_approval". Gate implementation: agentic_core/api/vsb.py:1598-1617 (_gate_status, _gates_blocking).
- **disclosed to the user:** Yes — each refusal carries its own mover, gate and reason.
- **refutation: REFUTED — corrected to DELIVERED.** REFUTED IN HALF, and the half that fails is the half the title leads with. The Mode-3 gate is DELIVERED and I stand behind it without reservation: six independent movers refused with 409 and distinct per-mover wording, the establish-time ship refused itself and said why, a DCS-sealed approval released all of them, /evolve proposes rather than mutates, and output-formats separates eight live renders from six unproduced targets instead of implying mp4. That is real §17.4 Mode-3 behaviour verified by execution, tier 0. But 'the repository is a real version-controlled, downloadable whole' must be struck. The assessor reached 27 files only by hand-POSTing /repo again after the ship; the ship route itself — §13's canonical act — leaves a 13-file manifest, a 13-file zip asserting 'x-repo-missing: none', and 16 of the founder's own generated surface files unreachable (index 0, which I reproduced twice). Those two claims are in direct conflict and cannot both be DELIVERED. Reporting them bundled lets the strongest thing in R2 launder the worst. Corrected verdict DELIVERED at tier 0 for the review gate alone; the §13 'coherent, downloadable whole' claim belongs to index 0 as a tier-1 defect, not here as delivered. Minor note for the register: only 4 git commits were written for 5 shipped surfaces (board_pack produced none), so the per-surface commit lineage is not quite complete either.

Then POST on each mover -> 409 x6 with a per-mover reason: 'repo refused', 'ship refused', 'website refused', 'web app refused', 'phone app refused', 'board pack refused', each with {mover, gate:'design', status:'pending', blocks_progress:true}.
