## R6 — §8 + §12 + §17.5 — the biomimetic living organism, the economic organism, the ten architecture invariants

**Assessor's region summary:** R6 (§8 biomimetic organism · §12 economic organism · §17.5 the ten invariants), measured against the live backend at 127.0.0.1:8031 from HEAD 6e63e762 by executing routes and reading handlers, components and stores.  THE HEADLINE. The economic organism's accounting core is genuinely sound and I verified it by execution: two cycles, a period close and an inter-VSB transfer, with the trial balance and balance sheet balanced at every step, the approved six-stage waterfall applied exactly, both hash chains verifying against their tail anchors, and virtual-WST honesty on every payload. The organism's disclosure discipline is, on most surfaces, the best thing in the region — /organism/status proves 60% of its own health figure is a default and a simulation; /organism/selection REFUSES and names each unmeasured measure; the cadence records "could not run" as distinct from "nothing was due"; the venture allocator states "recorded, unfunded — the investee is NOT credited". That is the standard the rest should be held to, and three places fall below it.  THE THREE TRUTH DEFECTS. (1) Every deliverable the platform produces carries a quality record asserting that the Endocrine regulator "holds a real HomeostaticRegulator with an integral term", while that file's own docstring records that no integral accumulates and no derivative term exists — FU-307's fix reached the docstring and a comment in the SAME FILE as the false string, but not the string that ships. (2) The CFO cash-flow statement's net movement is structurally always zero (cash in exactly equals cash out in every cycle, and transfers post against reserve_fund, never cash), so the VSB Economy page printed "0 WST net" for a period in which 500 WST verifiably left the entity — with no caveat, where the sibling trial_balance carries a careful scope string. (3) The period close and the board pack are credited to a "CFO agent (AI C-Suite)" when there is not one model call anywhere in the 2,002-line economy module, and the bare attribution is sealed into the append-only chain where it cannot be corrected.  WHAT IS PARTIAL AND SILENT. The Owner's four-cause charity directive does not bind: Dawah receives zero while two causes the Owner never named take 37% of the budget, and the payload lists the four priorities directly above the contradicting grants without saying a named priority got nothing. The six-stage recirculation loop (P3.16) can never run — auto_metabolic has no route, no config key and no UI toggle — yet the Heartbeat page calls it "OFF by default", which tells a reader it is a setting.  I DID HIT THE TEN-FINDING CAP, AND TEN IS NOT THE SIZE OF THE GAP. Not reported above, each verified: `operating_costs` is a caller-declared input echoed back as if measured, with no "declared" label; `capital_preserved: true` is published bare on /api/v1/economy/status although its real meaning is only "the capital_fund share is > 0" (the UI explains this, the API does not); the POST /heartbeat/beat record omits the `could_not_run` facts that /status carries, so a beat reports only what it did; the CCA review response omits the twin pre-validation verdict it just computed (the record and both UIs carry it); `/api/v1/economy/status` publishes `metabolic_energy` bare; `change_control/regulator.py` holds a `diversity_score` computed from `time.time()` and four repair functions returning `repair_status: "recovered"` / `integrity: 1.0` for faults nothing repaired — unreached today only because the loop above cannot be enabled; the §17.5 GaaS figure has drifted (7 of 63 modules, not 8 of 57); and the §17.5 status paragraph now UNDERSTATES the system on torch optionality and control-perimeter isolation, both of which I drove and found holding. A reader should take these ten as the most consequential, not as all there was.

### R6.0 · Every delivery's quality record tells the reader the Endocrine regulator has an integral term; that file's own docstring says it has none — **PARTIAL** *(assessed DOC_OVERCLAIM)* · tier 1

- **severity (assessor):** HIGH
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** A reader of any produced deliverable is shown the sentence "agentic_core/biomimicry/geospheric/regulator.py holds a real HomeostaticRegulator with an integral term", and the layers_note immediately above it says "`layer_basis` names the module behind each claim" — directing the reader to trust it. The file itself records the opposite: "`integral_error` is assigned once below as a dict of zeros and never accumulated, so there is no integral term". This is not an omission and not an environment effect: it is a positive assertion about a named module's mechanism that the module contradicts, on a surface a person reaches. It is above tier 2 because nothing on the surface qualifies it — the adjacent `layer_states` value `code_exists_unreached` says the layer does not RUN, which a reader reads as real code one wiring away, exactly the impression the false clause reinforces.
- **claim:** §17.2 names Endocrine (signal bus / shared context + MJM) as one of the seven biomimetic layers, and §8 W512 recorded the measured finding that `geospheric/regulator.py` "is **not a controller**: its `integral_error` is declared and never accumulated, its `dt` is discarded, its corrective actions are strings", with `vbs/quality.py` named as the one surface built to tell the truth about these layers (registered FU-307, Tier 1).
- **observed:** FU-307's correction reached the regulator's docstring (W515) and a comment at `agentic_core/vbs/quality.py:514` ("NOT a PID regulator"), but NOT the string that actually ships. `LAYER_STATE["Endocrine"]["basis"]` at quality.py:59-63 still claims an integral term, and `assure_delivery` publishes `biomimetic.layer_basis` on every quality record — reached from `api/deliverables.py`, `api/genesis.py`, `api/products.py`, `api/resource_fabric.py`, `api/swarm.py` and `api/management_systems.py`. This is the "fix every writer, not one" class, with the surviving writer being the reached one.
- **evidence:** Executed: POST http://127.0.0.1:8031/api/v1/deliverables/produce (HTTP 200) returned, at `quality_assurance.biomimetic.layer_basis.Endocrine` and again at `versions[0].quality_assurance.biomimetic.layer_basis.Endocrine`: "agentic_core/biomimicry/geospheric/regulator.py holds a real HomeostaticRegulator with an integral term and NOTHING IMPORTS IT; a second copy of the same class sits in geospheric/homeostatic_regulator.py." Contradicted by C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/biomimicry/geospheric/regulator.py:12-18 ("NOT PID CONTROL, and this docstring used to say it was (FU-307, W515). Measured on this file: `integral_error` is assigned once below as a dict of zeros and never accumulated, so there is no integral term; `dt` is computed in regulate() and never read, so there is no derivative term") and by C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/vbs/quality.py:514 in the same file as the false string. Writer: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/vbs/quality.py:59-63; publisher: quality.py:556 (`biomimetic["layer_basis"]`).
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the surrounding fields (`layer_states: code_exists_unreached`, `layers_note`) qualify REACHABILITY, not the mechanism claim, and the note explicitly points the reader at `layer_basis` as the authority.
- **refutation: STOOD, verdict CORRECTED DOC_OVERCLAIM → PARTIAL.** NOT REFUTED, tier UNCHANGED at 1. I reproduced the exact false clause on a reached surface and confirmed it is false of both copies of the named class. Tier 1 rather than 2: this is not an omission or an environment effect but a positive assertion about a named module's mechanism, and the sentence directly above it tells the reader layer_basis is the authority for each claim; the adjacent `code_exists_unreached` qualifies REACHABILITY ("real code one wiring away"), which reinforces rather than corrects the mechanism claim. Not tier 0/3 because nothing on the surface walks it back — the same file's own comment at quality.py:514 says "NOT a PID regulator", so the module contradicts itself and only the reached half lies. VERDICT CORRECTED DOC_OVERCLAIM -> PARTIAL: the rubric's DOC_OVERCLAIM is for the vision or a canon doc claiming more than the system; here the vision's §8 W512 record is CORRECT and the overclaim lives in shipped runtime code published on every quality record. The capability (per-layer state reporting, six honest states, an accurate `layer_states` map) genuinely works and one of its seven basis strings asserts an untruth — PARTIAL with the shortfall UNDISCLOSED. If the register prefers to treat the shipped LAYER_STATE table as canon-in-code, DOC_OVERCLAIM is defensible; the tier is 1 either way.
- **refuter's evidence:** Reproduced by execution: POST http://127.0.0.1:8031/api/v1/deliverables/produce (HTTP 200) returns at quality_assurance.biomimetic.layer_basis.Endocrine the string "agentic_core/biomimicry/geospheric/regulator.py holds a real HomeostaticRegulator with an integral term and NOTHING IMPORTS IT; a second copy of the same class sits in geospheric/homeostatic_regulator.py", immediately after layers_note ends with "`layer_basis` names the module behind each claim." Writer: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/vbs/quality.py:59-63; publisher quality.py:556-557. Contradicted on the named file: `grep -n integral agentic_core/biomimicry/geospheric/regulator.py` returns ONLY the docstring denial (lines 13-14, 38) and `self.integral_error = {k: 0.0 for k in self.setpoints}` at line 26 — there is no `+=` and no accumulation anywhere in the file. The duplicate at geospheric/homeostatic_regulator.py has no integral field at all, so the clause is false of both copies. The other two clauses ARE true: `grep -rn HomeostaticRegulator` (excluding _archive) finds only the two class definitions and quality.py's own strings — nothing imports it, and the second copy exists.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** In agentic_core/vbs/quality.py:60, replace "holds a real HomeostaticRegulator with an integral term" with what the file records — a proportional threshold comparison that names a corrective action as a string, with no integral and no derivative term — and add a guard asserting that no LAYER_STATE basis string claims a term the named module does not compute.

### R6.1 · The CFO cash-flow statement reports net cash movement that is structurally always zero, and cannot see the only account whose balance actually moves — **PARTIAL** · tier 1

- **severity (assessor):** HIGH
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The VSB Economy page prints a card headed "Cash flow · this period" showing "0 WST net" in a period in which 500 WST demonstrably left the entity (reserve_fund 4,000 → 3,500, confirmed by the transfer response's own `sender_reserve_fund_after_wst: 3500.0`). That is a financial-statement figure asserting an outcome the books contradict, with no caveat anywhere on the card or in the payload — unlike `trial_balance`, which carries a `scope` string explaining exactly what it is and is not comparable with. It is tier 1 rather than tier 2 because the card is not merely incomplete: it states a number, 0, which is false for the period shown, and the number cannot be anything else — in a complete cycle, cash in (revenue) exactly equals cash out (reserves + five distributions + declared costs), so net movement is 0 by construction, and every movement that is not a cycle bypasses `cash` entirely.
- **claim:** §12 (W446): "double-entry books with period close and CFO statements"; the close handler's own docstring claims "P&L · balance sheet · cash flow computed from the REAL double-entry postings".
- **observed:** `statements()` derives cash flow only from postings that touch the `cash` account (`receipts = _sum(lambda p: p["debit"] == "cash")`, `payments = _sum(... p["credit"] == "cash")`). The cycle's compat postings route everything through `cash` in equal and opposite totals, and the inter-VSB transfer posts `Dr transfer_out / Cr reserve_fund` — never touching `cash`. Measured on the live books, the `cash` account balance is exactly 0.0 after two cycles, a close and a transfer. Before the close the card read receipts 20,000 / payments 20,000 / net 0, where 4,000 of those "payments" were an internal move into the entity's own reserve fund, not an outflow. The P&L and balance sheet are correct and balanced throughout; the cash-flow statement is the one of the three that carries no information.
- **evidence:** Executed: POST /api/v1/economy/transfer {from workstation-idbo, to vsb-a42ca383af, 500} → `sender_reserve_fund_after_wst: 3500.0`. Then GET /api/v1/economy/board-pack?vsb_id=workstation-idbo → `cash_flow: {"operating_receipts_wst": 0, "operating_payments_wst": 0, "net_cash_movement_wst": 0}` while `balance_sheet.assets.reserve_fund: 3500.0` (was 4000.0). Raw store C:/tmp/m1data2/data/economy/workstation-idbo_ledger.json: last posting `{debit: transfer_out, credit: reserve_fund, amount: 500.0, source: inter_vsb_transfer}`; `accounts.cash: 0.0`. Code: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/economy/ledger.py:635-650 (receipts/payments) and 315-324 (`_COMPAT_POSTING`, every pot paired against `cash`). UI: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/VSBEconomy.tsx:688-692 renders "Cash flow · this period" / `net_cash_movement_wst` with no basis line.
- **disclosed to the user:** No — `trial_balance` carries a `scope` string and `balances` carries a `balances_note`; `cash_flow` carries neither, and the UI card adds none.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). NOT REFUTED, tier UNCHANGED at 1. The structural claim is stronger than the assessor stated and I verified it from the posting list: `cash` is a pure transit account (Dr total == Cr total == 20,000, balance 0.0), every cycle pairs each pot against it so net movement is 0 by construction, and every non-cycle movement (close, inter-VSB transfer) bypasses it entirely. Tier 1 rather than 2: the card headed "Cash flow · this period" asserts a FIGURE, 0 WST net, in a period where the entity's only liquid asset fell 4,000 -> 3,500 and the same payload's own P&L reports net -500 for that period — a financial-statement number that is false for the label it is given, not a blank field and not a missing capability. Not tier 2 because the defect is an assertion rather than an undisclosed partiality, and the sibling trial_balance proves the codebase knows how to caveat a figure of this kind and did not. VERDICT PARTIAL retained: the P&L, balance sheet, trial balance and close are genuinely correct and balanced throughout (see index 9), so the shortfall is one of the three statements, undisclosed at both the payload and the UI card.
- **refuter's evidence:** Reproduced: GET /api/v1/economy/board-pack?vsb_id=workstation-idbo returns statements.cash_flow = {operating_receipts_wst: 0, operating_payments_wst: 0, net_cash_movement_wst: 0} for period 1 (from_posting 20), in the SAME payload as profit_and_loss.net_profit_wst: -500.0 and balance_sheet.assets.reserve_fund: 3500.0. I dumped all 21 postings from C:/tmp/m1data2/data/economy/workstation-idbo_ledger.json: postings 0-13 are two cycles in which cash is debited 10,000 and credited 2,000+1,600+2,400+1,600+1,200+1,200 = 10,000 (total cash Dr 20,000 = total cash Cr 20,000); postings 14-19 (period_close) touch retained_earnings only; posting 20 is `transfer_out / reserve_fund 500.00 inter_vsb_transfer` and never touches cash. Stored `accounts.cash: 0.0`. Code: ledger.py:635-637 (receipts = Dr cash, payments = Cr cash), ledger.py:315-324 (_COMPAT_POSTING pairs every pot against cash). Caveat search: `grep -rn cash_flow` over agentic_core and apps (excluding _archive) returns exactly TWO hits — the writer at ledger.py:649 and the render at VSBEconomy.tsx:691 — so no basis, note or scope string exists anywhere, unlike trial_balance, which carries a `scope` sentence in the same payload.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Give `cash_flow` a `scope`/`basis` string the way `trial_balance` has one, stating that it sums movements through the `cash` account only and that inter-VSB transfers and reserve movements post against `reserve_fund` and are not visible here — or, better, derive the statement from the movement of every asset account so the figure means what its name says. Render that basis on the VSBEconomy card.

### R6.2 · A period close and the board pack are credited to a "CFO agent (AI C-Suite)" that does not exist on the path, and that attribution is sealed into the append-only audit chain — **PARTIAL** · tier 1

- **severity (assessor):** MEDIUM
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The reader of the close response and of the board pack is shown `prepared_by: "CFO agent (AI C-Suite)"`, and the hash-chained UEG record stores the bare form with no qualifier at all. No agent prepared these statements: there is not a single model call anywhere in `agentic_core/api/economy.py`, and `close_period()` is arithmetic over stored postings. This is not the AI_DISABLE_LOCAL environment speaking — it is a field asserting an actor, and no actor acted; the platform would make the same claim with models fully enabled, because nothing on this path ever calls one. It is above tier 2 because it is an assertion about who produced a financial statement, not a missing capability, and above tier 3 because nothing on the surface says the attribution is nominal. The audit chain is append-only, so the fabricated attribution cannot be corrected, only superseded.
- **claim:** §12 (W446): "double-entry books with period close and CFO statements"; §17.5 invariant 3, append-only DCS audit, which makes whatever is written permanent.
- **observed:** `agentic_core/economy/ledger.py:652` stamps `prepared_by: "CFO agent (AI C-Suite)" — computed from the double-entry postings` onto the statements (the trailing clause partly qualifies it), and `agentic_core/api/economy.py:1638` writes the BARE string `"prepared_by": "CFO agent (AI C-Suite)"` into the tamper-evident UEG close event with no qualifier. A grep for `gateway` or `query_meta` across the whole 2,002-line economy module returns zero hits, so no C-Suite resource is consulted on any economy path.
- **evidence:** Executed: POST /api/v1/economy/close-period {vsb_id: workstation-idbo} → HTTP 200, `statements.prepared_by: "CFO agent (AI C-Suite) — computed from the double-entry postings"`. The sealed record in C:/tmp/m1data2/data/ueg.json, event_201: `{"type": "economy.period_close", "vsb_id": "workstation-idbo", "net_profit_wst": 4000.0, "retained_earnings_wst": 4000.0, "prepared_by": "CFO agent (AI C-Suite)", "disclaimer": "Virtual/simulated WST — no real funds moved."}` with `previous_hash`/`hash` chained. Code: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/api/economy.py:1638; C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/economy/ledger.py:652. Measured absence: `grep -n "gateway\|query_meta" agentic_core/api/economy.py` → no output.
- **disclosed to the user:** Partly — the statements payload appends "computed from the double-entry postings", which a careful reader can take as the real method. The UEG record and the economy.py writer carry no qualifier at all.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). NOT REFUTED, tier UNCHANGED at 1. Tier 1 rather than 2: the field names an ACTOR for a financial statement and no actor acted — `close_period()` is arithmetic over stored postings and the module contains no model call on any line, so the platform would make the identical claim with models fully enabled. That removes the AI_DISABLE_LOCAL defence entirely: this is not "no model ran" as environment, it is an assertion of authorship the code can never satisfy. Not tier 3 because nothing on the UEG record qualifies it, and the append-only chain makes the fabricated attribution permanent and only supersedable. The statements-payload trailing clause "computed from the double-entry postings" is a partial mitigation on ONE of the two writers and does not reach the sealed one. VERDICT PARTIAL retained: the close itself is real and correct (verified at index 9); the attribution is the shortfall.
- **refuter's evidence:** Reproduced from the sealed record rather than by re-closing: C:/tmp/m1data2/data/ueg.json node event_201 = {"type": "economy.period_close", "vsb_id": "workstation-idbo", "net_profit_wst": 4000.0, "retained_earnings_wst": 4000.0, "prepared_by": "CFO agent (AI C-Suite)", "disclaimer": "Virtual/simulated WST - no real funds moved."} with previous_hash and hash chained, and GET /api/v1/gaas/ueg/verify returns valid: true over 441 events with anchor_state present — so the bare attribution is inside a verified, append-only chain. Live GET /api/v1/economy/board-pack returns statements.prepared_by: "CFO agent (AI C-Suite) - computed from the double-entry postings". Writers: agentic_core/api/economy.py:1638 (bare) and agentic_core/economy/ledger.py:652 (qualified) — `grep -rn prepared_by` over agentic_core and apps returns ONLY those two, so no UI reads it and no third writer qualifies it. Measured absence of any actor: `grep -n "gateway|query_meta|served_by|invoke_native" agentic_core/api/economy.py` returns ONE hit, line 1355, which reads provenance off a stored run for an unrelated route — the close path makes no model call at all. For contrast I confirmed the CCA path DOES call `gateway.query_meta` (change_control.py:1351) and labels its fallback honestly, which is what this path does not do.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Change both writers to `prepared_by: "the ledger's own close routine — deterministic arithmetic over the double-entry postings; no C-Suite resource was consulted"`, and grep for the other `prepared_by`/`assembled_by`/`curated_by` strings that name an AI tier on a path with no model call.

### R6.3 · Dawah, one of the Owner's four named charity causes, receives nothing while two causes the Owner never named take 37% of the budget — **PARTIAL** · tier 2

- **severity (assessor):** MEDIUM
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The cycle response prints `priorities: ["clean_water", "conflict_relief", "dawah", "orphan_sponsorship"]` and, directly beneath it, a grant list containing clean_water, conflict_relief, orphan_sponsorship, famine_food and emergency_health. A reader is shown four priorities and five grants and is nowhere told that one of the four named priorities received zero, nor that two of the five grants are to causes outside the directive. Every individual sentence in the payload is true — the method string, the weights_provenance, the amounts — so this is not tier 1; it is tier 2 because the shortfall (the Owner's directive does not bind the allocation) is real and the surface does not state it. It is above tier 3 because the surface actively juxtaposes the priority list with an allocation that contradicts it, rather than saying "a named priority received nothing".
- **claim:** §12: "donates intelligently to causes by real-world urgency (WATER · Orphan · Conflict/disaster · Dawah; 100%-donation-only)" and, W446, "the four causes with a 100%-donation-only screen and Owner-set directives (inclusions / exclusions honoured)".
- **observed:** `CharityIntelligence.score` applies a flat `+0.12` priority boost capped at 1.0 over a seven-cause editorial pool, then `allocate(budget, top=5)` takes the top FIVE by score. Dawah's curated inputs (urgency 0.55, marginal_impact 0.675) put it 7th of 7 at 0.8482 even after the boost — below famine_food (0.8987) and emergency_health (0.8642), neither of which is in the directive. The cap also silently destroys the boost for the strongest causes (clean_water's base 0.88625 + 0.12 = 1.00625 → 1.0), so the Owner's instrument is simultaneously too weak to lift a named cause and inert on the causes already at the top. `priorities_source` is `never_set`, so these are the platform's editorial defaults standing in for the Owner — which the directives surface does say.
- **evidence:** Executed: POST /api/v1/economy/cycle {vsb_id: workstation-idbo, revenue: 10000} → `giving_back.priorities: [clean_water, conflict_relief, dawah, orphan_sponsorship]`; `giving_back.grants` = clean_water 252.77, conflict_relief 252.77, orphan_sponsorship 248.85, famine_food 227.16, emergency_health 218.44 — 445.60 of 1,200 WST (37.1%) to two non-directive causes and 0.00 to dawah. GET /api/v1/economy/charity/candidates?top=10 → dawah ranks 7th of 7 at score 0.8482. Code: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/economy/charity.py:199-205 (the +0.12 boost, `min(1.0, …)`), 206-214 (`ranked(top)`), 216-221 (`allocate` → `self.ranked(top)` with top=5).
- **disclosed to the user:** No. `weights_provenance` honestly says the weights are editorial and nothing is measured, and `donation_100pct_verified: "not_checked"` is honest about the screen — but no field says a named priority received nothing, or that grants went to causes outside the directive.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). NOT REFUTED, tier UNCHANGED at 2. Tier 2 rather than 1: every individual statement in the payload is true — the four priorities really are the directive in force, the five amounts really are pro-rata by score, `weights_provenance` admits the weights are editorial and `donation_100pct_verified: not_checked` admits the screen did not run. Nothing asserts that dawah was funded. Tier 2 rather than 3 because the shortfall — the Owner's priority instrument does not bind the allocation, being a +0.12 nudge inside a top-5 cut that `method` never mentions — is real and no field, payload or card states it; I confirmed the juxtaposition is reachable on one UI screen, not just in a response body. VERDICT PARTIAL retained.
- **refuter's evidence:** Reproduced without re-running a cycle. GET /api/v1/economy/charity/candidates?top=10 returns all seven curated causes with scores: clean_water 1.0, conflict_relief 1.0, orphan_sponsorship 0.9845, famine_food 0.8987, emergency_health 0.8642, refugee_support 0.8487, dawah 0.8482 (7th of 7). Code: charity.py:199-205 (`base + 0.12` under `min(1.0, ...)`), 206-214 (`ranked(top)` sorts and slices), 216 (`allocate(budget, top=5)` -> `self.ranked(top)`), so the top-5 slice funds famine_food and emergency_health and drops dawah, a named priority. The cap is inert on the two leaders exactly as stated (clean_water and conflict_relief both land on 1.0). allocate()'s return dict (charity.py:252-266) carries `priorities: sorted(self.priorities)` and `grants` side by side plus `method`, `weights_provenance` and `donation_100pct_verified: not_checked` — and NO field stating that a named priority received nothing or that grants went outside the directive; `method` never mentions a top-N cut. metabolism.py:351 passes that dict through unchanged as the cycle's `giving_back`, so the cycle payload does not even carry the `priorities_note` that /charity/directives carries. Vision text confirmed at docs/WORKSTATION_IDBO_WHOLE_VISION.md:516-517 ("WATER · Orphan · Conflict/disaster · Dawah; 100%-donation-only"); _PRIORITIES defaults are those four and get_directives reports priorities_source never_set with an honest note (charity.py:87-103). UI: VSBEconomy.tsx:576-580 renders the grants list and the same page mounts <CharityDirectives/> (VSBEconomy.tsx:815), which renders the priorities field including dawah plus the ranked pool of seven (CharityDirectives.tsx:73, 133-143, 205) — so a user sees dawah named as a priority and absent from the grants on one screen, with nothing connecting the two.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Add to the allocation payload a `priorities_unfunded` list (named priorities that received 0) and a `grants_outside_directive` list, each with the score that put them there; and either make the priority set a filter rather than a +0.12 nudge, or say on the surface that the directive is a weighting hint, not a restriction.

### R6.4 · The six-stage recirculation loop can never be switched on: auto_metabolic has no route, no config key and no UI control, yet the surface calls it "OFF by default" — **PARTIAL** *(assessed API_ONLY)* · tier 2

- **severity (assessor):** MEDIUM
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The Heartbeat page shows a card headed "Metabolic cycle (six stages) — measured, and whether it delivered" whose body reads "the metabolic cycle has not run on this process, so there is no outcome to report — the loop is paced and OFF by default, and this is not a withheld emission". "OFF by default" tells a reader this is a setting they could change; there is no setting. The page offers five autonomy checkboxes and this is not one of them, and `configure()` does not accept the flag. That is a real shortfall the surface does not state, so tier 2; it is not tier 1 because "OFF by default" is literally true (it is off, and that is the default) — the untruth is an implication, not an assertion. It is not tier 3 because the surface's own wording actively misdirects a reader looking for the lever.
- **claim:** §8 (W482): the six-stage recirculation loop (Sense · Intend · Analyse · Act · Learn · Reflect) under `agentic_core/avatars/core/`, with the rule that "an engine computes or says it cannot" — plan item P3.16, whose point is that the loop RUNS.
- **observed:** `OrganismHeartbeat.__init__` sets `self.auto_metabolic = False` and `self._metabolic_every = 10` (heartbeat.py:180,182). `configure()` (heartbeat.py:1010-1015) accepts only interval_seconds, auto_evolve, auto_economy, auto_align, auto_compliance, auto_ship — and `ConfigureRequest` (api/heartbeat.py:57-63) declares the same six. A repository-wide grep for `auto_metabolic` outside the integration suite returns only the two assignments in heartbeat.py and the gate at heartbeat.py:439. The only writers that set it True are in `integration_tests/test_mvp_spine.py`. So on a live system the beat's step 2g is dead code, and with it the whole P3.16 clearance/recirculation path — including the `RecirculationPreflight.intercept` self-healing branch whose repair functions return `repair_status: "recovered"` / `integrity: 1.0` for a fault nothing repaired (change_control/regulator.py:40-52), which is therefore a fabrication on a surface nobody can reach.
- **evidence:** Executed: GET /api/v1/heartbeat/status → `"last_metabolic": null`, `"metabolic_basis": "the metabolic cycle has not run on this process, so there is no outcome to report — the loop is paced and OFF by default, and this is not a withheld emission"`; three POST /api/v1/heartbeat/beat calls returned `actions: ["pulse", "homeostasis", "transformation_tick"]` with no metabolic entry. Code: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/organism/heartbeat.py:180,182,439,1010-1027; C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/api/heartbeat.py:57-71; UI card C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/organism/HeartbeatMonitor.tsx:188-200 (whose own comment asserts "The loop has run from the beat since W533"), autonomy list rendered at HeartbeatMonitor.tsx:126-152 ("All five are OFF by default").
- **disclosed to the user:** No — the surface says "OFF by default", not "this platform exposes no way to turn it on".
- **refutation: STOOD, verdict CORRECTED API_ONLY → PARTIAL.** NOT REFUTED, tier UNCHANGED at 2. I went one step further than the assessor and checked the persistence path: auto_metabolic is absent from _AUTONOMY_KEYS, so there is no file, env, route or checkbox by which any user or operator can switch the loop on — it is reachable only from the test suite. Tier 2 rather than 1: "the loop is paced and OFF by default" is literally true (it is off, and that is the default), so the untruth is an implication — the phrase "by default" tells a reader a setting exists — not an assertion. Tier 2 rather than 3 because the surface's own wording misdirects a reader hunting for the lever, and the page shows five checkboxes none of which is this one; a disclosed gap would have to say "this platform exposes no way to turn it on". VERDICT CORRECTED API_ONLY -> PARTIAL: API_ONLY means a backend exists that no UI reaches, and would tell a reader a curl could enable the loop. No API reaches it either, so API_ONLY understates the gap; PARTIAL (the beat runs and reports honestly; one of its steps is unreachable code) is the accurate label. The self-healing fabrications behind the gate stay tier 3 on their own, as a surface nobody reaches.
- **refuter's evidence:** Reproduced. GET /api/v1/heartbeat/status returns last_metabolic: null and metabolic_basis: "the metabolic cycle has not run on this process, so there is no outcome to report - the loop is paced and OFF by default, and this is not a withheld emission"; the status payload's auto_* keys are exactly auto_ship, auto_evolve, auto_economy, auto_align, auto_compliance — no auto_metabolic. `grep -rn auto_metabolic` over the whole tree (excluding _archive) returns 9 hits: heartbeat.py:180 (assignment False), heartbeat.py:439 (the gate), and SEVEN in integration_tests/test_mvp_spine.py. No route, no config key, no env var. configure() (heartbeat.py:1010-1015) accepts six named flags and not this one; ConfigureRequest (api/heartbeat.py:57-63) declares the same six; and _AUTONOMY_KEYS (heartbeat.py:955) is exactly ("auto_evolve", "auto_economy", "auto_align", "auto_compliance", "auto_ship"), so even hand-editing the persisted organism_autonomy.json cannot set it — _load_autonomy only reads those five keys. UI: HeartbeatMonitor.tsx:39-51 defines AUTONOMY as those same five, line 128 prints "All five are OFF by default", and lines 196-200 print s.metabolic_basis verbatim under the heading "Metabolic cycle (six stages) - measured, and whether it delivered". I also confirmed the unreachable fabrication behind the gate: change_control/regulator.py:66-74 returns repair_status "fixed"/"reconciled"/"excised"/"recovered" and integrity 1.0 from functions whose bodies only reshape the fault dict, and line 81 sets diversity_score = 0.35 + (0.05 * time.time() % 1); the only importer chain is governance/uci_interceptor.py -> avatars/core/recirculation_orchestrator.py, which the beat reaches only through the dead gate.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Either add `auto_metabolic` to `configure()`/`ConfigureRequest` and to the autonomy list (it is already paced at 1-in-10 beats), or change `metabolic_basis` to say the loop is off and that no surface can enable it, naming P3.16 as the work — and add a guard asserting that every flag the beat gates a step on is reachable from `ConfigureRequest`.

### R6.5 · composite_health is published as a bare number on two surfaces while /organism/status goes to great length to say 60% of its weight is a default and a simulation — **PARTIAL** · tier 2

- **severity (assessor):** MEDIUM
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** `GET /api/v1/plan/state` returns `"organism_health": 1.0` and `POST /api/v1/organism/homeostasis` returns `"composite_health": 1.0`, each with no terms, no `measured` flags and no basis. `GET /api/v1/organism/status` returns the same family of figure with `composite_health_terms`, `composite_health_basis`, `composite_health_measured_weight` and an explicit sentence that the blended figure "cannot be read as a measurement". The two bare readers are the FU-405 class — the basis did not travel with the number — so a consumer of either surface is given a health figure it cannot qualify. Tier 2 rather than tier 1 because, at the moment I measured, the measured-only score really was 1.0, so no shown number was false; the defect is that these readers will publish the blended figure (0.885 was live on /organism/status at 14:46Z) as bare "health" with no way for a reader to know 60% of its weight is a 1.0 default and an ATP simulator. Not tier 3 because the shortfall is in what the reader is told about a figure they are shown, not a missing capability.
- **claim:** §8: "profitability, customer/user satisfaction, founder-alignment, and live compliance are all continuously monitored, evaluated, and improved" — and §15.6, honesty over polish, never fabricate, label simulation.
- **observed:** `organism_health` on /plan/state is `immune.status()["health"]` (one of the three terms) published under a whole-organism name — the naming invariant: a quantity carrying a broader name than the law that computed it. /organism/homeostasis publishes `composite_health` straight from the blend with no terms. Both sit beside /organism/status, which is exemplary: it states which 40% is measured, that self-healing is "defaulted to 1.0, not a measurement", that metabolic is "ATPSimulator on a constant metabolic_load — simulated", and that the mode decides on the measured-only score.
- **evidence:** Executed: GET /api/v1/plan/state → `{"organism_health": 1.0, "threat_level": "NOMINAL", …}` (no basis field anywhere in the payload). POST /api/v1/organism/homeostasis → `{"homeostasis_triggered": true, "composite_health": 1.0, "adjustments_made": [], "adjustments_basis": "0 of 0 entr(y/ies) changed something…"}` — the adjustments carry a basis, the health figure does not. Contrast GET /api/v1/organism/status at 14:46:26Z → `composite_health: 0.885`, `composite_health_measured_only: 1.0`, `composite_health_basis: "40% of this figure's weight is measured (immune); the rest is a self-healing term defaulted to 1.0 and a simulated metabolic term…"`. Source of /plan/state's figure: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/api/transformation.py:69-73 (`d["organism_health"] = immune.status().get("health")`).
- **disclosed to the user:** No on /plan/state and /organism/homeostasis; fully disclosed on /organism/status and /organism/health-summary.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). NOT REFUTED, tier UNCHANGED at 2. Tier 2 rather than 1 for the reason the assessor gave and I confirmed by measurement: at the time I read them the immune term and the measured-only composite were both 1.0, so no figure shown was false. The defect is that /plan/state and /api/v1/board/status publish the IMMUNE term under the name organism_health, and /organism/homeostasis publishes the BLENDED figure bare — so a consumer is handed a health number with no terms, no measured flag and no basis, and cannot know that 0.885 (live on /organism/status earlier in the day) is 60% a 1.0 default and an ATP simulator. Tier 2 rather than 3 because the shortfall is in what the reader is told about a figure they are shown, not a capability that is missing — and /organism/status proves the platform computes the disclosure and simply does not ship it with the number. VERDICT PARTIAL retained; the finding should name three bare readers, not two.
- **refuter's evidence:** Reproduced, and I found a THIRD bare writer the assessor missed. GET /api/v1/plan/state returns {"organism_health": 1.0, "threat_level": "NOMINAL"} and the payload's full key list (reconciled_at, api_routes, resources, resource_compositions, vsb_entities, vsb_recent, last_evolution_cycle, evolution_items_proceeding, board_directors, board_directives, organism_health, threat_level) contains no basis field of any kind. POST /api/v1/organism/homeostasis returns composite_health: 1.0 beside adjustments_basis "0 of 0 entr(y/ies) changed something..." — the adjustments carry a basis, the health figure does not. THIRD: GET /api/v1/board/status returns live: {"organism_health": 1.0}, written at agentic_core/api/board.py:340 (`snapshot["organism_health"] = immune.status().get("health")`) — the same single-term immune figure under the same whole-organism name. Source of /plan/state's figure confirmed at agentic_core/api/transformation.py:71-72. Contrast verified live: GET /api/v1/organism/status returns composite_health 1.0, composite_health_measured_only 1.0, composite_health_measured_weight 0.4, composite_health_terms naming immune (weight 0.4, measured true), self_healing_health (weight 0.4, measured false, "no circuits tracked yet - defaulted to 1.0, not a measurement") and metabolic (weight 0.2, measured false, "ATPSimulator on a constant metabolic_load - simulated"), plus composite_health_basis stating that 40% of the weight is measured. Reachability note I checked: BoardOfDirectors.tsx:30 declares live.organism_health in its Status interface and no line renders it, so these three bare figures are reached as response bodies, not as rendered cards.
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Make both bare readers carry the same `composite_health_terms` + `composite_health_basis` the status route already computes (one shared helper, so a third reader cannot drift), and rename /plan/state's field to `immune_health` since that is the law that computed it.

### R6.6 · The Sovereign Evolution page renders truncated floor echo under a highlighted "BTO Transformation Roadmap" heading with no provenance badge on the card — **PARTIAL** · tier 3 *(assessed tier 2)*

- **severity (assessor):** MEDIUM
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The card is titled "BTO TRANSFORMATION ROADMAP" in highlight colour and prints the raw string, which contains "## Transformation Roadmap\n  - NOW (thi." and "- Offering: what is delivered and the scope boundary." and a term-frequency list — prompt-shaped filler, not a roadmap. The only disclosure travels INSIDE the body text (the floor marker on line 1, "(deterministic scaffold)", "The list below is the request's most frequent terms, not findings about them"), and the card itself carries no provenance badge even though the codebase has `provenanceBadge` for exactly this. This is the "a badge in the DOM is not a label on the text" class in reverse: the label is buried in the payload the reader must parse, not attached to the claim. Tier 2 and not tier 1 because the text does disclose what produced it; tier 2 and not tier 3 because the card's framing — a highlighted governance heading over fragments like "- NOW (thi." — invites a reader to take scaffold text as the organism's plan.
- **claim:** §8 (W446): "the Sovereign Evolution Office introspects → AI CEO triage → C-Suite verdicts → CoE/BTO roadmap → Change Control, on the circadian heartbeat" — the roadmap is the CoE/BTO output of that cascade.
- **observed:** The cycle itself is admirably honest about every OTHER field: `ceo_directives[0].source: "default"` with `source_basis: "NOT ISSUED BY THE AI CEO - the CEO call returned no line this parser could read"`, `items_unevaluated: 1` with `items_basis: "…an unevaluated directive is not a decision to proceed"`, `curated_by_basis` naming exactly what the CoE and BTO prompts returned, `change_control_submissions: []`. Only `bto_roadmap` is handed to the UI raw, and only that card is rendered without a provenance line.
- **evidence:** Executed: POST /api/v1/sovereign-evolution/cycle → `bto_roadmap` begins "_[Workstation native structured engine — owned, no external dependency]_\n\n_Acting as: Centres of Excellence._\n\n## CoE Quality Gate\nStructured service frame for: - [COO | maintenance | P2 | effort not sized] Routine organism health review  (NO C-SUITE VERDICT - not evaluated)" and later "## Transformation Roadmap\n  - NOW (thi." plus "- transformation roadmap / - business transformation / - bullets transformation". Render: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/evolution/SovereignEvolution.tsx:274-281 — `<h3>BTO Transformation Roadmap</h3>` then `{roadmap.bto_roadmap}` in a `whitespace-pre-wrap` paragraph, with no `provenanceBadge` call anywhere in the file's roadmap card.
- **disclosed to the user:** Partly — the floor marker and the "most frequent terms, not findings" clause are inside the rendered text; the card carries no badge and no "the deterministic floor served this" line of its own.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). NOT REFUTED, but TIER CORRECTED DOWN 2 -> 3, and I am flagging the direction explicitly. The gap is real: the §8 cascade's CoE/BTO output is prompt-shaped scaffold with a truncated heading echoed as content, not a roadmap. But the stated tier rule is that a shortfall the surface itself states is tier 3 however large, and this surface states it in the text the reader actually reads: the card's FIRST line is the floor marker, the body says "(deterministic scaffold)" verbatim, and it says the list is "the request's most frequent terms, not findings about them". A reader is told what produced this and that it is a frame rather than analysis. It is not tier 2 because nothing is hidden from the reader of that card, and not tier 1 because no figure or verdict asserts an outcome the cascade did not produce. The genuine weaknesses I would keep in the register are that the disclosure travels only inside the payload text while the card itself carries no badge, that "native structured engine - owned, no external dependency" is a softer phrase than the badge helper's own "structured floor - not model analysis", and that the stored roadmap loses its served_by so the page's one badge degrades to "provenance not recorded". VERDICT PARTIAL retained, with the shortfall DISCLOSED in-body.
- **refuter's evidence:** Reproduced from the STORED roadmap (GET /api/v1/sovereign-evolution/roadmap, the endpoint the page loads), so no new cycle was run. bto_roadmap begins, as its first rendered line, "_[Workstation native structured engine - owned, no external dependency]_", then "_Acting as: Centres of Excellence._", then "## CoE Quality Gate ... (NO C-SUITE VERDICT - not evaluated)", then the truncated echo "## Transformation Roadmap\n  - NOW (thi.", then "## Transformation Roadmap / Structured phased frame (deterministic scaffold):", and under Governance "The list below is the request's most frequent terms, not findings about them." followed by "- transformation roadmap / - business transformation / - bullets transformation". The rest of the cycle is honest exactly as described: curated_by_basis = "the CEO call returned no directive this parser could read, so the maintenance default stood in; 0 of 1 directive(s) carry a parsed C-Suite verdict; the CoE and BTO sections are the text those two prompts returned", items_unevaluated 1, change_control_submissions []. Render confirmed at SovereignEvolution.tsx:274-281: <h3> "BTO Transformation Roadmap" in highlight, then {roadmap.bto_roadmap} in a whitespace-pre-wrap <p>, with no provenance call in that card. Separately I found that the page's one provenance badge sits on the earlier Organism Introspection card (SovereignEvolution.tsx:191-198) and, because the stored roadmap carries served_by/any_external/calls = None, provenanceMapBadge renders "provenance not recorded" (api.ts:84-86) rather than "structured floor - not model analysis".
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Attach a provenance badge to the roadmap card from the cycle's own `served_by` (the helper exists at apps/workstation-superapp/src/lib/api.ts), and have the Sovereign Evolution Office return `bto_roadmap_served_by` / `bto_roadmap_is_analysis: false` as fields rather than leaving the disclosure to be read out of the prose.

### R6.7 · Two of the ten §17.5 invariants declared absolute are not held in code: no KPI gate exists anywhere, and only 7 of 63 API modules construct the constitutional gate — **PARTIAL** · tier 3

- **severity (assessor):** MEDIUM
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** The §17.5 status paragraph in my own region states both gaps plainly ("KPI gate before customer delivery: nothing gates any delivery on KPIs (OWNER RULING P3.0 — build or amend)"; "GaaS gate on every output: true for 8 of 57 API modules — every other output passes only a three-regex keyword filter"), and `/api/v1/gaas/status` is scrupulous at the surface, stating that its breaker covers only the sovereign-node interceptor, that every other governed path builds its own, that "none of those is reflected here and no surface aggregates them" and that "This is not a platform-wide constitutional health figure." So however large the gap, a reader is told about it — tier 3 by the stated rule. It is not tier 2 because I found no surface and no doc sentence that claims either invariant is met; it is not tier 1 because no figure or verdict asserts gating that did not happen.
- **claim:** §17.5: "The 10 architecture invariants (absolute). User isolation · mandatory GaaS gate on every output · append-only DCS audit · Arms-Length Agency · digital-twin pre-validation before major change · torch optionality · single router-mount point · signal-bus atomicity · living business plan ≤5-min staleness · KPI gate before customer delivery."
- **observed:** KPI gate: a repository-wide search for `kpi_gate`, `KPI_GATE`, `require_kpi`, and for KPI combined with gate/block/refuse/deliver across `agentic_core/` returns ZERO non-archive hits. No delivery path consults a KPI. The §17.5 figure for the GaaS gate is also now stale in the other direction: I measure 7 of 63 API modules constructing `UnifiedConstitutionalInterceptorV16Omega` (board, constitutional_gaas, economy, forge, genesis, swarm, transformation_orchestration), and 18 of 63 importing anything from `agentic_core.gaas` at all — not 8 of 57. Of the other eight invariants, I verified six hold by execution or code (see the DELIVERED finding).
- **evidence:** Executed: GET /api/v1/gaas/status → `circuit_breaker_scope: "the 'sovereign-node' interceptor only … none of those is reflected here and no surface aggregates them. This is not a platform-wide constitutional health figure."` Measured: `grep -rn "kpi_gate|KPI_GATE|require_kpi" --include=*.py agentic_core/` → no output; `grep -rn "KPI" agentic_core/ | grep -i "gate|block|refus|deliver"` (excluding _archive and tests) → no output. `grep -ln "UnifiedConstitutionalInterceptorV16Omega" agentic_core/api/*.py | wc -l` → 7, against 63 modules in agentic_core/api/. No surface or page in apps/ or agentic_core/ contains the phrase "every output" as a gating claim.
- **disclosed to the user:** Yes — §17.5's own status paragraph names both, and /api/v1/gaas/status refuses to present its breaker as platform-wide coverage.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). NOT REFUTED, tier UNCHANGED at 3. Tier 3 and not 2 because I searched for a contradicting claim and found none: no handler, payload, page or canon sentence asserts that a KPI gates delivery or that GaaS gates every output — §17.5's own status paragraph says the opposite in both cases, and /api/v1/gaas/status volunteers that its breaker is not platform-wide. Tier 3 and not 1 because no figure or verdict reports gating that did not happen. One correction to the finding that strengthens rather than weakens it: §17.5's figure "8 of 57 API modules" is stale in the system's DISfavour — I measure 7 of 63 constructing the interceptor (11%, not 14%) — so the doc slightly overstates coverage inside an otherwise honest disclosure. That is a doc-staleness rider, not a reason to re-tier, because the sentence still tells the reader the invariant is only partly held. VERDICT PARTIAL retained for the invariant set: eight of ten hold (six verified at index 9), two do not.
- **refuter's evidence:** Every measurement reproduced. `grep -rn "kpi_gate|KPI_GATE|require_kpi" --include=*.py agentic_core` (excluding _archive) returns NOTHING; `grep -rni kpi agentic_core/*.py` filtered to gate|block|refus|deliver returns only four unrelated lines (api/genesis.py:1049 a kpi string in a plan template, api/vbs_systems.py:149 and vbs/qms.py:78 and vbs/quality.py:280, all comments about the cockpit's coverage gate). No delivery path consults a KPI. Counts: agentic_core/api holds 63 .py modules; 7 construct UnifiedConstitutionalInterceptorV16Omega; 18 import anything from agentic_core.gaas. GET /api/v1/gaas/status is scrupulous at the surface, returning circuit_breaker_covers (two named paths) and circuit_breaker_scope: "the 'sovereign-node' interceptor only ... Every other governed path constructs its own interceptor per call with its own breaker (board-node, economy-node, forge-node and others); none of those is reflected here and no surface aggregates them. This is not a platform-wide constitutional health figure." Disclosure confirmed in my own region: docs/WORKSTATION_IDBO_WHOLE_VISION.md:840-851 states "Not held - ... KPI gate before customer delivery: nothing gates any delivery on KPIs (OWNER RULING P3.0 - build or amend)" and "GaaS gate on every output: true for 8 of 57 API modules".
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Correct §17.5's GaaS figure to the measured 7 of 63 (it is re-measured at every milestone and has drifted), and take the P3.0 Owner ruling on the KPI gate — build it or amend the invariant list from ten to nine, because an invariant declared absolute that nothing enforces is a declared domain nobody enforces.

### R6.8 · The organism still cannot turn over or select: no apoptosis, autophagy, senescence or death exists anywhere, and two of §8's four measures have no mechanism — **MISSING** · tier 3

- **severity (assessor):** MEDIUM
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** Every surface that touches these two verbs refuses rather than pretends, and says why in its own words. `/api/v1/organism/selection/{vsb_id}` returns `status: "NOT_ASSESSABLE"`, `selected: null`, and names each missing measure with a basis that distinguishes absence from a low reading ("This is the absence of a signal, not a low one - nothing is counted as a zero and no default stands in for silence"); `/floor` returns `floors_not_assessable` and states that its result "does not clear the entity on them". `organism/genome.py`'s own docstring states nothing in it evaluates fitness. So the gap is total and the disclosure is complete — tier 3 by the stated rule, however large the missing capability. Nothing here fabricates a fitness, a satisfaction score or a selection verdict, which is what would push it to tier 1.
- **claim:** §8 (W512): the organism "does not yet regulate, turn over or select"; "No apoptosis, no autophagy, no mitosis, no senescence and no death… A system that cannot remove or replace its own parts cannot be said to heal" — against §3's "self-healing, self-improving organism" and §8's four continuously-monitored measures (profitability, customer/user satisfaction, founder-alignment, live compliance).
- **observed:** Turnover: a case-insensitive search for apoptosis / autophagy / mitosis / senescence across `agentic_core/`, `apps/` and `core/` (excluding _archive) returns exactly two hits, both in `agentic_core/change_control/regulator.py` — `mitosis_scale` (clones a template dict; NO caller anywhere in the tree) and `meiosis_recombine`, whose `diversity_score` is computed as `0.35 + (0.05 * time.time() % 1)`, a number from the clock. There is no death, no removal and no replacement of any part. Selection: of §8's four measures, `user_satisfaction` and `founder_alignment` have no mechanism anywhere, and the selection module says so in those words; `profitability` and `live_compliance` are only readable for entities on the living roster. Regulation: the endocrine loop is the subject of finding 1; the metabolic term is a simulator that `/organism/status` itself states "can_fall: false" with the arithmetic proof.
- **evidence:** Executed: GET /api/v1/organism/selection/workstation-idbo → `status: "NOT_ASSESSABLE"`, `unmeasured: [profitability, user_satisfaction, founder_alignment, live_compliance]`, `basis: "SELECTION REFUSES: 4 of the 4 measures §8 names are not measured for this entity…"`, `method: "…No verdict, score or ranking is produced here"`. GET …/floor → `floors_not_assessable: [live_compliance, section_8_obligations]`. GET /api/v1/organism/status → `systems.metabolic.can_fall: false` with the full arithmetic basis. Measured: `grep -rni "apoptosis|autophagy|mitosis|senescen" --include=*.py --include=*.tsx agentic_core/ apps/ core/` (excluding _archive) → 2 hits, both C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/change_control/regulator.py:85,91; `grep -rn "mitosis_scale"` → only its own definition. Fabricated figure: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/change_control/regulator.py:81 (`child["diversity_score"] = 0.35 + (0.05 * time.time() % 1)`), reachable from no live caller.
- **disclosed to the user:** Yes, and unusually well — the selection and floor surfaces refuse and name every missing measure; the only unreached fabrication (`diversity_score` from the clock) sits behind a loop no surface can enable.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). NOT REFUTED, tier UNCHANGED at 3. Tier 3 and not 2 because every surface that touches turnover or selection REFUSES in its own words and names each missing measure, distinguishing an absence from a low reading and declining to substitute a proxy for the founder's judgement; the reader of those surfaces is told the gap is total. Tier 3 and not 1 because nothing fabricates a fitness, a satisfaction score or a selection verdict on any reachable path — the one fabricated figure I confirmed, diversity_score from time.time(), sits behind the dead auto_metabolic gate of index 4 and no live caller reaches mitosis_scale or meiosis_recombine at all. VERDICT MISSING retained: the capability §3 and §8 name (an organism that heals by removing and replacing its own parts, and selects on four continuously-monitored measures) has no implementation, and that is disclosed rather than pretended.
- **refuter's evidence:** Reproduced. `grep -rni "apoptosis|autophagy|mitosis|senescen" --include=*.py --include=*.tsx agentic_core apps core` (excluding _archive) returns exactly TWO hits, both in C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/change_control/regulator.py — line 85 `async def mitosis_scale` (clones a template dict into _template_cache) and line 91 its UEG log string. Nothing in the tree removes or replaces a part. meiosis_recombine at regulator.py:80-83 sets child["diversity_score"] = 0.35 + (0.05 * time.time() % 1), a figure from the clock. Selection refusal verified live: GET /api/v1/organism/selection/workstation-idbo returns status NOT_ASSESSABLE, selected null, unmeasured [profitability, user_satisfaction, founder_alignment, live_compliance], each with its own basis — founder_alignment's reads "NOT MEASURED: founder-alignment has no mechanism anywhere in this platform. §8 names it as one of four continuously-monitored measures and nothing records it ... It is not substituted by a proxy", and user_satisfaction's reads "This is the absence of a signal, not a low one - nothing is counted as a zero and no default stands in for silence" — with method ending "No verdict, score or ranking is produced here". GET /api/v1/organism/status reports systems.metabolic as measured false, "ATPSimulator on a constant metabolic_load - simulated, not a measurement of this platform".
- **smallest honest fix (assessor's proposal — a lead, not a decision):** Register the `diversity_score` clock figure and the `repair_status: "recovered"` constants in `change_control/regulator.py` for removal or for an `assessable: false` return before anything wires that path, and keep the selection surface's refusal as the model for the user-satisfaction mechanism FU-311 needs — a measure built behind the A.9.5 boundary, never a composite dressed as selection.

### R6.9 · Verified by execution: the double-entry books balance through cycles, a close and a transfer, both audit chains verify, and four invariants §17.5's status text still records as not held now hold — **DELIVERED**

- **severity (assessor):** NONE
- **why it is this tier (assessor):** No tier — each claim below was confirmed by executing the route or by driving the condition, not read off a status field. I am reporting it because the honest picture needs it: the economic organism's accounting core and the audit invariants are genuinely sound, and §17.5's status paragraph now UNDERSTATES the system in two places, which is its own kind of staleness.
- **claim:** §12: "double-entry books with period close and CFO statements"; §17.5 invariants 1 (user isolation), 3 (append-only DCS), 5 (twin pre-validation), 6 (torch optionality), 7 (single router mount), 8 (signal-bus atomicity).
- **observed:** Double-entry integrity holds end to end. Two cycles of 10,000 WST, a period close and a 500 WST inter-VSB transfer: the trial balance stayed balanced at every step (20,000/20,000, then 4,000/4,000, then 3,500/3,500), the balance sheet balanced at every step, the close posted real closing entries and rolled 4,000 into retained_earnings, the next period opened clean at 0 postings, and the waterfall applied the approved split exactly (Stage 0 reserve 20%, then Owner 20 · Self-investment 30 · Capital Fund 20 · User-projects 15 · Charity 15 of the distributable 8,000). Virtual-WST honesty is on every payload. Both audit chains verify with their tail anchors present, so truncation and rollback are ruled out. Twin pre-validation really does run on a HIGH change and labels itself honestly. The signal bus is lock-guarded with a bounded ring. All 85 `include_router` calls are in `app_mvp.py`. And two §17.5 status claims are now stale in the system's favour: torch optionality HOLDS (the app imports with torch blocked at the meta path, where the status says "the app fails to import without torch"), and the organism's control perimeter IS auth-scoped (heartbeat, genome, organism-status, sovereign-evolution and board are mounted under `_require_admin`, where the status says they "carry no auth dependency").
- **evidence:** Executed, in order: POST /api/v1/economy/cycle ×2 (revenue 10000) → `homeostasis_reserves: 2000.0`, `distributable_profit: 8000.0`, circulation owner 1600 / self_investment 2400 / capital_fund 1600 / user_projects 1200 / charity 1200. POST /api/v1/economy/close-period → `close: {posting_index: 20, net_profit_wst: 4000.0}`, `retained_earnings_wst: 4000.0`, `trial_balance.balanced: true`. GET /api/v1/economy/board-pack → `period: {from_posting: 20, postings: 0, opened_after_close: 1}`, `balance_sheet.balanced: true`. POST /api/v1/economy/transfer (500) → trial balance 3500/3500, balanced. GET /api/v1/ueg/verify → `chain_valid: true, entries: 5, tail_anchored: true`; GET /api/v1/gaas/ueg/verify → `valid: true, events: 305, anchor_state: "present", verified_basis: "every stored hash recomputed, and the chain head matches the tail anchor, so truncation and rollback are ruled out"`. POST /api/v1/cca/submit {change_type: genome_edit} → tier HIGH; POST …/review approve → record carries `twin_prevalidation: {verdict: "pass", source: "health_gate_default", source_label: "no twin model — health gate only", method: "…The proposed change was NOT simulated"}`, rendered with that label at apps/workstation-superapp/src/pages/enterprise/ChangeControlAgency.tsx:330-341 and BoardOfDirectors.tsx:233-234; POST …/implement → HTTP 409 (Board ratification required). Torch: blocking `torch` on `sys.meta_path` and importing `agentic_core.app_mvp` → "APP IMPORTS WITHOUT TORCH: OK". Mounts: C:/Users/rehan/Workstation/agentic_core/app_mvp.py holds all 85 `include_router` calls, with `dependencies=[_Depends(_require_admin)]` at lines 368, 372, 415, 423, 439. Bus: agentic_core/organism/nervous.py:51-52 (`deque(maxlen=SIGNAL_WINDOW)`, `threading.Lock()`), biobus.py:147,507.
- **disclosed to the user:** Yes — and the payloads over-deliver on disclosure: `balances_note`, `cycles_posted_basis`, `trial_balance.scope`, `energy_state`, `funding_state: recorded_unfunded` with "the investee is NOT credited", and `real_money_rails: DISABLED` are all honest about exactly what the figures are and are not.
- **refutation: SURVIVED** (reproduced by the refuter). NOT REFUTED and tier 0 UNCHANGED — but ONE claim in it over-reaches and I am correcting it rather than passing it. The sentence "the organism's control perimeter IS auth-scoped" is supported only for the five routers it names (app_mvp.py lines 368, 372, 415, 423, 439 mount genome, organism-status, sovereign-evolution, board and heartbeat under _Depends(_require_admin)). Of the eight surfaces §17.5 names, CCA (line 383) and swarm (line 318) carry _get_current_user, which is user-level and not admin-level, and business-plan (line 455) is mounted with NO router dependency at all — business_plan.py has 9 @router handlers and exactly 1 Depends(_get_current_user) — while living_plan (line 427) has 3 handlers and no auth anywhere. So §17.5's status sentence is stale for most of the perimeter but not all of it, and the DELIVERED claim should be narrowed to the five admin-mounted routers. The torch half of the staleness claim is fully verified by driving the condition. Everything else listed reproduced by execution, so DELIVERED with no tier is right: the economic organism's accounting core and the audit invariants are genuinely sound, and reporting that is part of the honest picture.
- **refuter's evidence:** Independently re-verified, not taken on trust. Double-entry integrity: I dumped all 21 postings of workstation-idbo's ledger and the waterfall is exact — Dr cash 10,000 / Cr revenue, then reserve 2,000 (20%), then owner 1,600 / self_investment 2,400 / capital_fund 1,600 / user_projects 1,200 / charity 1,200 = 8,000 distributable, twice; the close posted six real closing entries (revenue -> retained_earnings 20,000, then the five expense roll-ups) and recorded closes: [{posting_index: 20, net_profit_wst: 4000.0}]; the live board pack reports trial_balance balanced true at 4,000/4,000 and balance_sheet balanced true at 3,500/3,500 after the transfer. Both chains verify live: GET /api/v1/ueg/verify -> chain_valid true, 7 entries, anchor_state present, tail_anchored true; GET /api/v1/gaas/ueg/verify -> valid true, 441 events, anchor present, "every stored hash recomputed, and the chain head matches the tail anchor, so truncation and rollback are ruled out". Single router mount: 85 include_router calls in app_mvp.py, and the only other file matching include_router is api/cross_platform.py, where the match is a DOCSTRING line, not a call — so the invariant holds. TORCH OPTIONALITY HOLDS: I drove the condition by inserting a MetaPathFinder that raises on torch and torch.*, then imported agentic_core.app_mvp -> "APP IMPORTS WITHOUT TORCH: OK, routes: 531". Twin pre-validation is real and honestly labelled in code: change_control.py:1351 calls gateway.query_meta, 1374 falls back to verdict/source = (pass|fail, health_gate_default), 1391-1392 attach "was NOT simulated" and source_label "no twin model - health gate only", 1874 blocks implementation naming that label. Bus atomicity: nervous.py:49-52 holds deque(maxlen=SIGNAL_WINDOW=200) plus threading.Lock().
- **residual lead (assessor's note on a DELIVERED entry — a lead, not a defect):** Re-measure and correct the §17.5 status paragraph: torch optionality and the control-perimeter isolation have been delivered since it was written, and an invariant list whose status text understates the system trains readers to discount it.
